How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

(part 5/96)

# Changelog/2026/June 24
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 24] SkyBlock Resource Pack, Loadouts, and Healing Revamp Changes
Updated: June 24, 2026
Hello!
06/24 Changes
- Reduced the cooldown from the wardrobe, equipment wardrobe, power stones and tuning points from 30 seconds back to 0 seconds
Designer Note: As mentioned below in the thread, this change was purely dependent on player feedback and we quickly realized that there are quite a lot of situations a 30 second cooldown applied to everything would simply cause too many issues for now. However, we still think that a Hotm/Hotf cooldown is necessary, which is why from now on loadouts and hotm/hotf slots themselves will apply a 30 second cooldown on swap. If you don't swap your hotm/hotf loadouts from loadout to loadout, then that cooldown won't apply.
06/29 SkyBlock Resource Pack Changes
- Added custom icons for all stats and mob types in the game
- Removed the in-hand models for these items:
  - Wither Blades
  - Midas' Sword
  - Mythological Weapons
  - Tormentor
  - Cleavers
  - Yeti Sword
- Mythological Weapons have been completely redesigned
- Cleavers have been completely redesigned
- Yeti Sword has been slightly redesigned
- Improved Jerry-chine Gun texture
- Anti-Sentient Pickaxe looks less like a sword now
- Simplified the textures of Longswords
- Most Wands have been given a more distinct look
- Summoning Eyes have changed to look closer to their in-game model
- The shapes of Jasper and Ruby Gemstones have been swapped
- Minor changes to the following items:
  - Ragnarock
  - Sword of Bad Health
  - Shadow Fury
  - Livid Dagger
  - Adaptive Blade
  - Blade of the Volcano
Today on the Alpha Network we've got the first version of the SkyBlock Resource Pack and a new Loadouts system, which includes the highly requested Equipment Wardrobe. Further changes have also been made to the Healing Revamp update.
You'll need to be on Minecraft version 1.21.11 or higher in order to join this Alpha session, although we recommend using version 26.2 instead.
## ► SkyBlock Resource Pack
Today, we have something a little different on the Alpha Network: the first version of the official SkyBlock Resource Pack! This initial version of the pack contains 800+ textures for various items in SkyBlock, with more to come in the future. Here's a gallery with some of them:
These are only some of the textures included in this release

### ➥ Resource Pack FAQ
What Minecraft version do I need to be on? You must be on at least Minecraft version 1.21.11 or higher in order to join the Alpha Network.
With the release of Minecraft 26.2 recently, we are dropping support for versions 1.21.9 and 1.21.10. Please update soon if you haven't already. In a few weeks' time, we'll also drop support for 1.21.11, so we recommend players migrate to 26.1 or 26.2 instead.
Am I required to use the resource pack? Yes, once the pack is added to the Main Network, it will be force-enabled there. Many pieces of future content coming soon will use custom models, textures, and menus. It would not be feasible for us to support an entirely different version of the game that didn't use the resource pack.
If there are any textures for specific items that you aren't a fan of, please leave your feedback below as always.
Am I allowed to override the SkyBlock Resource Pack with mods? Yes, although please keep in mind that all mods are considered use-at-your-own-risk. Additionally, bear in mind that a significant portion of items in SkyBlock have been turned into vanilla Paper items on the backend to help prevent placing animations/exploits, so doing this will make items indistinguishable from each other.
When will <item> get a custom texture made for it? This is just the first version of this resource pack; the goal is for most items in SkyBlock to eventually get a texture. We expect to update the SkyBlock Resource Pack quite frequently.
Things like armor and pets are all things we would like to tackle in the future, but will require more time and care to get right. We are starting with more basic items and weapons for texturing first, as they are what you see in your hotbar and menus the most often.
<item> has an underwhelming texture. Is the team open to feedback? Yes! Here is a snippet from our Art Team, after reading the comments so far:
Hypixel Minecraft Artists: Hi everyone! We've noticed your questions and feedback about the art shared so far for the pack, and wanted to clarify.
What we have shown so far is an early, alpha test-ready version of the SkyBlock Resource Pack. To get the ball rolling on the pack, we started by texturing as many basic items as we could to showcase our ideas. Everything shown so far is subject to change as we work on trying to get this pack right. Please tell us what works and what doesn't work - the more specific the feedback, the better!
While our goal for the pack was to keep it close to vanilla Minecraft's style, we know we might miss sometimes. So, let's work together to make SkyBlock look its best! With all of that in mind, you might even notice we have started implementing changes based on your suggestions.
Will future updates come out with textured items in the SkyBlock Resource Pack? Going forward, yes, all new items except for Pets and Cosmetics should get textures on release. A few new items might slip through the cracks for the first month, but by and large, everything should be getting custom textures.
Where are the custom menus that were shown off a couple of years ago? Over the past several months, we've been rolling code to the game to allow us to properly implement custom menus, but the menus themselves will not be included in the first release. This first release will help us iron out style and stomp out any bugs. These textures are just the first steps on the road!
### ➥ Additional Changes
We've also made several changes to various items to support a few of the fancier resource pack features. Some of these features also double as small QoL improvements.
- Added a visual cooldown to Enderman Slayer Sword abilities.
- Added a visual cooldown between shortbow shots.
- Made watering cans show their water level as a durability meter.
- Updated several item abilities to use Item Display entities, and improved their animations. This affects the following abilities:
  - Bonemerang
  - Tribal Spear
  - Flower of Truth
  - Bouquet of Lies
  - Halberd of the Shredded
  - Giant's Sword
  - Time Shuriken
  - Extremely Real Shuriken
- Applied a unique tooltip texture to each item rarity.
- Updated the arrow hotbar display to show the texture of the arrow type you're using.
- Hid the empty tooltip on glass pane UI elements in The Forge.
## ► Loadouts
Switching builds is one of the clunkiest things you do in SkyBlock. Going from your combat setup to a fishing or mining one means bouncing between the Wardrobe for armor, the Pets menu for your pet, and the Accessory Bag to swap your Power and even your Magical Power tuning. Everytime you want to swap from one activity to another, there are bunch of menus that you have to go through before you can just start playing.
Loadouts lets you save a full build once and bring it back whenever you want with a single click.
### ➥ Feedback we're looking for
We are also looking for feedback on the following aspects of this update:
- We've added a cooldown to swapping Loadouts and other systems like the Wardrobe. Especially with all the newly added systems that now can all be swapped at the same time, there will most likely be some issues in the future unless we add a cooldown to these systems.
  - Previously, the wardrobe did not have a cooldown whatsoever which has led to a playstyle where people would often swap mid-combat to min-max their armor for specific parts of slayers, dungeons or kuudra for example. We want to move away from that playstyle and are looking for feedback regarding the cooldown duration.
  - Right now, the cooldown is set at 30 seconds on a system to system basis. So your wardrobe and equipment wardrobe have seperate cooldowns, but your loadout is on cooldown as long as anything else within the loadout is also on cooldown.
- We are also aware that there are currently obvious loopholes such as swapping armor from your hotbar/inventory, which we'll still have to decide on, so let us know your opinion below.
### ➥ What a Loadout saves​
Each Loadout allows you to save the following things: Armor, all 4 pieces Equipment, your Necklace, Cloak, Belt and Gloves Pet, whatever pet you select Accessory Power, your selected Power Stone for the Accessory Bag Tuning Points, your Magical Power tuning distribution Heart of the Mountain Heart of the Forest
The way it works is that your Loadout talks to the other systems in the game, like your Wardrobe, Tuning Templates, Pets, Equipment Wardrobe (new) and Hotf/Hotf Slots (new) and loads the stored data that you have already configured there. You select multiple of these setups and connect them to one loadout. This means that most things are not stored within the loadout itself, but in their seperate instances instead. This also allows you to use your Wardrobe, Equipment Wardrobe or Hotm/Hotf Loadouts seperately from the Loadout system aswell.
### ➥ How it works​
Open the new Loadouts menu and edit a loadout (replacing the Wardrobe menu in your Skyblock Menu, or /loadout) Here you can select for example your Armor which opens the Wardrobe Select one of your Wardrobe slots to save to your Loadout (which remain the same as they were before the update) You now have connected that Wardrobe slot your Loadout which means that when you load the Loadout the next time, it will always load that specific Wardrobe slot You can add more things to your Loadout, for example your Equipment which opens the new Equipment Wardrobe (/eq) Then rename the loadout to whatever you want so you can remember it better
And that's basically it. From now on everytime you load that loadout, it will load your Armor from Wardrobe and load your Equipment from the Equipment Wardrobe, or whatever else you might have added to it. P.S: You can also choose to select nothing, which keeps whatever you had before the swap!
### ➥ Equipment Wardrobe​
As part of this update, we have also added a Equipment Wardrobe. The new Equipment Wardrobe works the same as your current Wardrobe, but it saves your Equipment seperately from it. This way allows you to use your Equipment sets with any of your Armor sets without locking yourself to one specific composition.
### ➥ HotM/HotF Loadouts​
We also added Loadouts for your Heart of the Mountain and Heart of the Forest, allowing you to swap between different setups really easily.
### ➥ Other Loadout Changes​
Increased the default amount of wardrobe slots from 2 -> 4 Made the first tuning template free, refunding everyone that spent bits on it previously Added Community Upgrades for Loadouts, Hotf, Hotm and Equipment Slots Removed "Full Set First Worn" line from the wardrobe
## ► Healing Revamp Changes
We have been closely monitoring feedback regarding the Healing Revamp and are here to bring you a handful of changes along with some insight into our future plans for the update.
### ➥ Additions and Adjustments
- Added a Test Item 2 available in Centauri, giving you more tracking to spawn rare mobs.
- Various adjustments to mob damage have been made, including:
  - Dungeon Mobs
    - Most mobs have their damage reduced by 10%. The exceptions are:
    - 15% Reduction
      - F4/M4 Boss "animal" minions
        - Including Spirit Bear
    - 20% Reduction
      - Sadan's Giants
      - Sadan's Golems
      - Crypt Lurker
      - Scarf's boss minions
      - Skeleton Master
      - Skeletor
      - Skeletor Prime
      - Spirit Chicken
      - Super Archer
      - Zombie Commander
    - 25% Reduction
      - Teracottas
      - Frozen Adventurer
      - Shadow Assassin
    - 30% Reduction
      - Fels
    - Blaze Slayer
      - Boss (~-25% Damage) and Minibosses (a lot)
    - Enderman Slayer
      - Boss (~-30% Damage) and Minibosses (a lot?)
      - Broken Heart Radiation now deals 800 True Damage instead of a % of your health.
NOTE: We are aware of the request for wanting to be healed after fighting a boss, as the boss will leave you at low HP. Let us know if this is still needed after the damage reductions.
- Some item changes, including:
  - Explosive Arrows no longer damages yourself.
  - Destruction and Annihilation Cloak now heal 15/s and 25/s.
  - Ghast Cloak and Vanquished Ghast Cloak now heals 5/s and 15/s.
  - You can buy a Refrigerate III book for 1 Volcanic Rock + 50 North Stars
    - Previously, you can buy a Refrigerate I book for 1 Volcanic Rock + 10 North Stars, the change intends to reduce the amount of Volcanic rock needed.
  - Gloomlock Grimoire now requires 8 Enchanted Quartz Block to craft instead of 8 Enchanted Bone Block.
    - This is both to increase the obtainability of the item, but also since Quartz Blocks are more thematically fitting.
### ➥ Planned Adjustments
- Dungeon class changes, including:
  - Tank
    - Tank gets 10 + 0.5 Vitality per Class Level as part of its base stats.
  - Healer
    - Healer gets 10 + 0.2 Vitality per Class Level as part of its base stats.
    - Healer gets 10 + 0.5 Mending per Class Level as part of its base stats.
    - Mending also boosts the self-healing of Healers while in The Catacombs.
  - Berserk
    - Increased the melee damage boost from +0.4% per level to +0.75% per level.
    - Melee Hits increase the damage bonus from "Lust for Blood" 5x faster.
  - Mage
    - Mage now gets 5 Ability Damage + 1 Ability Damage every 5 levels as part of its base stats.
- Some HP% changes:
  - Fire Traps: 50% HP Damage → 15% HP Damage
  - Arrow Traps: 50% HP Damage → 15% HP Damage
  - Crusher: 100% HP Damage → 30% HP Damage
  - Lava Damage (applicable to F7, M7):
    - F7: 40% HP → 8,000 Regular Damage
    - M7: 40% HP → 60,000 Regular Damage
- Crimson Isle Miniboss Adjustments
  - Lowered kill-credit requirement from 1,000,000 to 250,000 damage.
  - Ashfang's Gravity Orb now deals 7,500 damage per second instead of 1-shotting the player.
  - Ashfang now heals 500,000 per absorbed Blaze instead of 2,000,000.
  - Ashfang's Blaze HP will be reduced to 2,000,000 HP.
  - Bladesoul's base damage reduced from 4,000 to 3,000.
  - Wither Plume's damage reduced to 7,500 (from practically a 1-shot).
  - DoT effects should no longer proc damage reflection from Mage Outlaw.
  - Barbarian Duke X's damage reduced from 4,500 to 2,000.
  - Barbarian Duke X's stun effect is reduced by ~40%.
  - Reduce the HP requirements of the Magma Boss fight by ~20%.
- Blaze Slayer Lava Pillars
  - You no longer need to use a Wisp Pet to kill lava pillars.
  - Using a Wisp pet and the Coldfused reforge will make killing lava pillars faster.
- /reclaim support for items where Ultimate Wise and Mana Disintegrators are now redundant.
There are more changes, especially damage changes, we are considering for Healing Revamp in the future, such as:
- Various instances of ability damages that aren't defined by a mob's base damage stat.
- Lava Fishing Minibosses
- Kuudra
- Diana mobs, if necessary.
- More item adjustments, if necessary.
We're aiming to give more insight into future adjustments at a later date; we've been prioritizing implementing the confirmed changes for testing first. Thank you for your patience during this process!
## ► Year 500 Raffle of the Century Event
The Year 500 Event is also still being tested, you can find all information about it on this thread.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2026/March 23/Alpha
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[March 23rd] Jerry Island QoL, New Abiphone Contacts, Reforge Anvil Removal, and more!
Updated: March 23, 2026
Hi everyone!
Today we've got an update containing some changes to Jerry Fishing, as well as the official discontinuation of Reforge Anvils within the game. Additionally, we have added several new Abiphone Contacts. Read on for all the details!
## ► Jerry Fishing Changes​
### ► Littlefoot's Den​
Littlefoot's Den is a new Glacite Mineshaft variant. If you are ever so lucky to happen upon one, you'll notice Littlefoot lingering around; it is his home, after all. This little guy packs quite a punch once he gets over his fear of outsiders, so come ready for a fight.
Once defeated or hunted, Littlefoot can drop a number of valuable items:
- Littlefoot Shard -> Grants +0.5 Gemstone Spread for every 29-20 Cold Resistance you have in the Glacite Mineshafts
- Littlefoot Fluff -> Used to craft the Pocket-Sized Igloo, which can upgrade a Baby Yeti Pet to MYTHIC rarity
Littlefoot also has a base 0.1% chance to replace any Glacite Maniac in any given Glacite Mineshaft.
Designer Note: We are looking into upping this base chance and adjusting Mythic Baby Yeti's perk as part of this Alpha session since we're not satisfied with its final design yet.
### ► Jerry Fishing QoL​
- Added Winter Sea Creature exclusivity - now you will only catch Winter Sea Creatures while on Jerry Island
- Added Walnuts to the Bazaar
- Added Fishing Hotspots to Jerry Island
- Adjusted Reindrake catch message to mention the player who caught it
- Adjusted some Winter Sea Creature weights & rarities:
  - COMMON Frozen Steve (5000 Weight)
  - UNCOMMON Frosty (2875 Weight)
  - RARE Grinch (1500 Weight)
  - EPIC Nutcracker (500 Weight)
  - LEGENDARY Yeti (100 Weight)
  - MYTHIC Reindrake (25 Weight)
    - Drop tables have also been updated due to certain SCs becoming a lot more common. They will be adjusted once again soon as part of the Magic Find Rework update. Most notably, the Baby Yeti Pet now drops as COMMON
- Added the ability for Double Hook Chance to activate on Reindrakes
- Adjusted some Jerry Fishing Bestiaries:
  - Moved Frozen Steve to Bracket 4, Tier 15 (1000 to max)
  - Moved Frosty to Bracket 4, Tier 15 (1000 to max)
  - Moved Grinch to Bracket 4, Tier 15 (1000 to max)
  - Moved Nutcracker to Bracket 4, Tier 15 (1000 to max)
  - Moved Yeti to Bracket - 5, Tier 15 (400 to max)
- Adjusted Fishing Level requirement for Frosty from Fishing Level 6 -> 1
- Adjusted Fishing Level requirement for Frozen Steve from Fishing Level 4 -> 1
- Adjusted Ice Bait and Winter Bait:
  - Ice Bait -> Grants +25 Fishing Speed and +4% Sea Creature Chance while on Jerry Island.
  - Winter Bait -> Grants +50 Fishing Speed and +8% Sea Creature Chance while on Jerry Island.
- Gregory will now spawn whenever a Reindrake does, ready to sell you Bows and Arrows for an extortionate price
- Removed the Winter Sea Creature buff from Icy Sinker now that you always catch Winter Sea Creatures
### ► Other Jerry Island QoL ​
- Added the ability to salvage the Icy Sinker for 20 Ice Essence
- Added a crafting recipe for the Hunk of Blue Ice
- Adjusted Ankylosaurus Pet's "Armored Tank" perk to grant 1-100% of your Defense as Strength
- Adjusted Baby Yeti Pet with the aim of making it more useful for Winter Fishing
- Adjusted the weight of certain items in Red Gifts:
  - Increased weight of LEGENDARY Snowman Pet from 1 -> 2 weight
  - Increased weight of Cryopowder Shard from 1 -> 4 weight
  - Increased weight of Winter Island from 1 -> 4 weight
- Adjusted Cryopowder Shard to now be part of a Crafting Recipe instead of applicable in an Anvil
- Adjusted Carnival Shop's "Icy Hook" perk to now grant 0.5-2.5% Sea Creature Chance while on Jerry Island
- Renamed True Ice Hilt to True Ice
- Moved Frozen Alex to a more visible/accessible location
## ► Abiphone Contacts and Changes​
Udel and Alda have been in fierce competition for some time now when it comes to who can sell the most Abiphones. Their travels have taken them all throughout SkyBlock, and have led to the following NPCs now owning their very own Abiphones. Why don’t you try and add them all to your contacts?
Spoiler: New Abiphone Contacts
- Agatha
- Bartender
- Bulvar
- Carrolyn
- Clerk Seraphine
- Dalir
- Elizabeth
- Frozen Alex
- Gemma
- Junker Joel
- Kysha
  - If Kysha is to be believed, a new Sea Creature might also have been added to the game, complete with some valuable drops…
- Mad Redstone Engineer
- Malik
- Marigold
- Moby
- Pesthunter Phillip
- Rosemary
- Shaggy
- Spider Tamer
- Talbot
- Taylor
- Terry
- Tony
- Tyzzo
- Vulcan
### ► Misc Abiphone Changes​
- Increased Abiphone Trios Cap from 15 → 25
- Improved Abiphone Trios Itemlore
- Increased rarity of Arachne Crystal from RARE → EPIC
- Adjusted some Abiphone Contact difficulties
- Added TAB autocomplete support to the /call command
- Added Travel Scroll to the Glowing Mushroom Cave to Moby’s Shop
- Removed the 7 rare relics quest from the Archaeologist
### ► Reforge Anvil Removal​
Starting today, we are beginning the process of removing Reforge Anvil functionality from Hypixel SkyBlock, for the purposes of simplicity. Everything you used to need a reforge anvil for (applying reforges, recombobulators, etc) can now be done in a regular anvil.
- Replaced the Reforge Anvil recipe at Obsidian Collection IX with +1 Mining Fortune & +50,000 Mining XP
- Removed Reforge Anvil purchase from Smithmonger
- Removed the Reforge Anvil from Malik's menu
- Removed all Reforge Anvil NPCs from Private Islands
- Added a 2m coin NPC sell price to Reforge Anvils
- Added +1 Mining Fortune to Obsidian Collection V, VII, and VIII
- Adjusted Reforge Anvil itemlore to show its sole purpose is now to be NPC sold
- Adjusted itemlore of all "Combinable in Reforge Anvil" items to now say "Combinable in Anvil".
- Converted all Reforge Anvils on Public Islands, such as the SkyBlock Hub, into regular Anvils
To prevent players from abusing the 2m NPC sell price of the Reforge Anvil on the main server while this update is still on Alpha, we have already implemented the changes to the Obsidian Collection that remove the Reforge Anvil's crafting recipe in addition to placing a 1 per SB Year cap on Reforge Anvil purchases from the Smithmonger.
## ► Other QoL​
As part of this update we also have an assortment of QoL changes to other areas of the game:
### ► Combat​
- Added /warp ci as an alias for /warp crimson isle
- Added several items to Slayer Sacks:
  - Golden Tooth
  - Revenant Viscera
  - Tarantula Silk
  - Null Ovoid
  - Molten Powder
  - Toxic Arrow Poison
  - Twilight Arrow Poison
- Added the ability to sell Mana Disintegrators to NPCs for 1,000 Coins
- Adjusted Bat Person Armor's passive to now only increase Combat Stats
- Adjusted Reaper Armor costs to now require 1x Revenant Catalyst instead of 64x Revenant Viscera in the top slot
- Allowed for Fire Aspect & Venomous enchantments to affect Mythological mobs
- Improved the Egg Sac holograms of the Tarantula Broodfather to make shooting them more intuitive
- Improved how Mob Types were spread throughout the SkyBlock Guide
- Reduced amount of kills needed to max Harpy, Gaia Construct, Cretan Bull Bestiaries from 4,000 -> 1,000
- Renamed "Blazing" Attribute to "Infernal Ruler"
- Renamed "Spirit Axe" Attribute to "Woodland Ruler"
- Renamed "Gravel Skeleton" to "Flint Skeleton" and added Flint, Enchanted Flint to their drop table
### ► Farming​
- Added Biofuel to Mining Sack and Garden Sack
- Adjusted the colour of the Farming Skill in /stats from dark green to light green
### ► Fishing​
- Added the "Elusive" Mob Type to all LEGENDARY Sea Creatures that were lacking it
- Added more Sea Creature Chance to Backwater Armor & Backwater Equipment
- Adjusted Titanoboa Attribute to now effect all Fishing Islands instead of just the Backwater Bayou

### ► Hunting​

### ► Mining​

- Chisels and stacks of Suspicious Scrap are no longer removed from the Fossil Excavator after use
### ► Other​

### ► Bugfixes​

# Changelog/2026/March 31/Alpha
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[March 31] Harvest Feast Event
Updated: March 31, 2026
Today we are bringing a new yearly event for Hypixel SkyBlock to the Alpha Network: the Harvest Feast! This Farming-focused event will run every SkyBlock Year from the 1st of Early Autumn through the 31st of Late Autumn. Keep reading for all the details!
## Harvest Feasts
### ➥ Overview
Feast Chef Ted is cooking up a massive Communal Stew for everyone in SkyBlock, and he needs your help to season it! Harvest Feasts take place every SkyBlock Year for 3 months: Early Autumn, Autumn, and Late Autumn.
During each month, 3 crops will enter their "harvesting season". Harvesting these crops during this period has a small chance to drop Seasoning for the stew and a new crafting material unique to each crop.
### ➥ Seasonings
Seasoning is a RARE CROP that has a 1/2,250 chance of dropping from in-season crops when harvesting them. It's not a physical item—it'll automatically be donated to the Communal Stew in The Hub.
Each Seasoning you donate to the stew will contribute towards your Donation Tiers.
### ➥ Donation Tiers + Celestial Mason Jars
There are five Donation Tiers each Harvest Feast at 5, 25, 75, 150, and 250 Seasonings cumulatively donated. Each Donation Tier reached will earn you a Celestial Mason Jar, the most powerful Mixin in SkyBlock!
Hitting the max Donation Tier will also earn you a Commemorative Mason Jar for that event.
## Crop Drops
Outside of just dropping Seasoning, in-season crops will also have a 1/18,000 chance of dropping a new crafting material unique to that crop. Each crafting material is also considered a RARE CROP , much like Seasoning.
Each of these materials is used to craft or obtain 1-3 new items.
## Grand Feasts
### ➥ Overview
Mayor Finnegan now has a new perk: "Grand Feast"! This replaces the Harvest Feast with a version that lasts an entire SkyBlock Year, in addition to opening the Grand Bakery for its duration.
### ➥ Grand Bakery
During a Grand Feast, whenever you drop a Seasoning from an in-season crop, it'll also give you a Kernel—a new currency you can spend at the Grand Bakery in The Hub to "bake" new rewards!
- Freshly Baked Accessories
  - Grants +1-5% RARE CROP chance
  - Talisman: 25 Kernels
  - Ring: 100 Kernels + Talisman
  - Artifact: 250 Kernels + Ring
  - Relic: 500 Kernels + Artifact
  - Heirloom: 1,000 Kernels + Relic
- Feast I Books
  - Applied to Farming Tools
  - Grants +0.5-2.5% RARE CROP chance
  - Costs 25 Kernels
- Finn's Focaccia
  - Consumable
  - Grants +5% RARE CROP chance for the rest of the Grand Feast
  - Costs 50 Kernels
- Ted's Contact Baked In Bread
  - Unlocks Ted's Abiphone Contact
  - One of the three in-season crops will give 10% more Seasonings, he'll call you to let you know which one
  - Costs 500 Kernels
- Scott's Contact Baked In Bread
  - Unlocks Scott's Abiphone Contact
  - Allows you to open the Grand Bakery outside of the Grand Feast
  - Costs 500 Kernels
- Bread Bowl
  - Costs 10,000 Kernels
  - Is a super cool thing to give to somebody
### ➥ Removal of Pelt-pocalypse
With the introduction of Finnegan's new Grand Feast perk we're retiring his old "Pelt-pocalypse" perk. Talbot's Shop and the Horse Trapper Animal are now permanently available. In addition to this, we've slightly reworked the Trapper's Crest accessory. It now upgrades an additional time and buffs the amount of Pelts you drop to compensate for the removal of the Mayor perk.
## Other Changes
### ➥ Carnival Perks
There are now 4 new perks available in Doug's Event Shop at the Carnival to help out with your progression through the Harvest Feast:
- Natural Talent I-V : Grants a +1-5% chance to find Seasonings during a Harvest Feast
- Fortunate Feasting I-V : Grants +5-25 Farming Fortune during a Harvest Feast
- Feast Crashers I-III : Grants +2-6 Bonus Pest Chance during a Harvest Feast
- Leftovers I-II : Earn +1/+2 Celestial Mason Jars if you reach the final Donation Tier
### ➥ Farming Tool Type
We've recategorized all Hoes and Axes used for farming into a new "Farming Tool" item type. They now share the same reforges and enchantments for simplicity sake. As a direct result of this, we've removed the Sunder enchantment from the game as it was redundant with the functionality of the Harvesting enchantment, but for Axes.
- Wooden/Golden/Stone/Iron/Diamond Hoe
- Rookie Hoe
- Basic/Advanced Gardening Hoe
- Basic/Advanced Gardening Axe
- Euclid’s Wheat Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Gauss Carrot Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Pythagorean Potato Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Newton Nether Warts Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Turing Sugar Cane Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Eclipse Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Wild Rose Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Pumpkin Dicer 1.0/2.0/3.0
- Melon Dicer 1.0/2.0/3.0
- Cocoa Chopper
- Cactus Knife
- Fungi Cutter
- Binghoe
We've replaced the Sunder VI drop from Locusts with a new drop: Locust Larva.
### ➥ New Visitor: Taylor
Taylor can now show up on your Garden as a MYTHIC Visitor starting at Garden Level XIV. She'll offer the player one of two cosmetics: the Fast Food Barn Skin or the Jelly Greenhouse Skin.
If she's offering the Barn Skin, she'll ask for lots of Deepfries, Salted Sunflower Seeds, and Enchanted Baked Potatoes.
If she's offering the Greenhouse Skin, she'll ask for lots of Floral Gelatin, Melon Juice, and Compacted Wild Roses.
### ➥ Farming Reforge Stone Changes
- Blessed Fruit
  - Moved stats from current Reforge Bonus to base stats (aesthetic change)
  - New Reforge Bonus now grants a chance for you to drop Enchanted Crops when farming
  - Fixed issue with Farming Fortune stat being lower than intended for RARE items with this reforge
- Golden Ball
  - Moved Farming Fortune from Reforge Bonus to base stats (aesthetic change)
- Large Walnut
  - Is now applied to "Farming Tools"
  - Buffed Farming Fortune this reforge provides significantly
  - Removed Speed base stats
### ➥ Floating Crystal Changes
- Floating Crystals no longer have an effective radius, they apply their affect to your entire Private Island
- Adjusted the rarities for certain Floating Crystals
- All Floating Crystals now have a new and unique texture
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2026/May 14/Alpha
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[May 14th] Lotus Atoll Alpha Testing #2
Updated: May 14, 2026
Hi all,
Today we're back again with another round of testing for the Lotus Atoll. If you missed the previous test, please refer to THIS THREAD for a more in-depth introduction to the update and its various mechanics.
Since we last took the Lotus Atoll off the Alpha Network, we've been hard at working implementing your feedback and polishing up the island. Below is a list of key changes to keep an eye out for as part of this second testing session:
## ► May 14th Changes
### ➥ New Content
- Added Fishing Hotspots to The Park
- Added Researcher Jessica to the Lotus Atoll, who hints players at how to obtain Tewtil Shards
- Added 2 more levels to the Rabbit Barn
- Added the Frogspawn Bucket
  - Can be used to upgrade LEGENDARY Frog Pets -> MYTHIC
  - Grants the perk "Home Sweet Home" -> Increases your chance of catching GOLD and DIAMOND Trophy Frogs by +0.1-10%.
  - MYTHIC Frog also gains +5 Trophy Chance as a base stat and "Happy Tree Friends" additionally grants +2 Fishing Speed per Frog (max 10 frogs)
### ➥ Changes to Exploding Lily Pads
- Reduced the size required to explode Lily Pads from 10 -> 8
- Exploding Lily Pads now only begin to decay after 60s without being interacted with
- Exploding Lily Pads now drop 60x - 100x Lotus instead of 1x Silver Lotus
- When decaying, Exploding Lily Pads now decay linearly rather than a percent of it's current size
- Adjusted Tewtil Shard to aid with combining and exploding lily pads faster:
  - Punching Exploding Lily Pads is +5-50% more effective.
- Adjusted Lotum Shard to help get rarer critters/sea creatures from exploding lily pads:
  - Grants +1-10 Tracking on Exploding Lily Pads.
- Adjusted exploding Lily Pads to now spawn additional mobs/critters upon explosion:
  - 3-5 Sea Creatures will spawn
  - 10% chance for a Flipflopper
  - 5% chance for a Seashine
    - When a Lily Pad explodes, the ❃ Tracking of all players involved will be used to calculate the chances of ♣ Elusive spawns (Puddle Jumper, Frog Prince, Flipflopper, Seashine). This is capped at 400 ❃ Tracking (x5 base chances).
    - Sea Creatures spawned are assigned to random contributors to the Lily Pad; elusive critters can be hunted by any player or contributor.
### ➥ Changes to Chapters​
- Improved the descriptions of several Chapter tasks
- Adjusted Chapter tasks that require players to catch Sea Creatures to show the rarity of the relevant SC
- Moved “Craft Froggles” task from Chapter 1 -> 2
- Moved “Donate a Frogcoin” task from Chapter 5 -> 4
- Moved “Catch a Frog Prince” chapter from 5 -> 6
- Added a new Chapter 5 task “Catch a Seashine”
- Added a new Chapter 4 task “Catch a Flipflopper”
- Added a new Chapter 4 task “Catch a Sea Frog”
- Added a new Chapter 4 task “Catch a Highlands Frog”
### ➥ Changes to Critters
- Added nametags to Lotusfish and Lotums
- Improved the Lotum exhaust message
- Increased Lotusfish Shard from +0.5-5 Trophy Chance on Lotus Atoll -> +1-10
- Increased Lotusfish spawn cap from 15 -> 25
- Added certain Fishing Net requirements to catch larger critters
  - Medium+ -> Joydive, Lumisquid, Seashine, Flipflopper
  - Large -> Shellwise, Spike
### ➥ Changes to Sea Creatures
- Adjusted all Lotus Sea Creatures to unlock at Fishing 10
- Adjusted the reworked Sea Creature Guide to show individual island names
- Buffed Lotus Crown drop rate from 0.1% -> 0.25%
- Added Enchanted Ink Sacs as a 0.5% drop from Squids
- Adjusted the Barrel of Riches & Lotus Crown recipes to use more materials
- Added more sounds to the Puddle Jumper Sea Creature, and increased his dialogue hologram visibility
### ➥ Changes to Trophy Frogs
- Lowered required pity for Exploding Frog and Puddle Jumper:
  - 50 catches for GOLD
  - 300 catches for DIAMOND
- Added a message informing the player when a Wormhole closes
- Allowed for Silver, Gold, and Diamond Lotuses to be added into Personal Compactors
- Added Silver Lotus, Gold Lotus, Frogcoin, and Seared Escargot to Sacks
- Changed the Lotus Eater NPC into a regular sized frog
- Added a new “Lotus Highlands” zone to better illustrate when players are eligible for Highlands Frog
- It can also get quite windy up here at night
- Adjusted Spinosaurus Pet to make it specific to Trophy Fish and less reliant on its Primordial Fisher perk
- MVP++'s golden GG feature is now enabled for a few seconds after a player fishes a Diamond Trophy for the first time
### ➥ Bug Fixes
- Reworded the Fishing Speed stat to make it more clear how the stat affects Fishing
- Fixed Atoll Croaker not dropping enough Lotus
- Fixed some bad Puddle Jumper jump locations
- Fixed some of the zoning of Tewtil Tunnel
- Fixed some Frogs not wanting to path find outside of water
- Added some dialogue to Puddle Jumper when you try to attack him (please don't)
- Fixed a visual issue with the Peace Treaty armor set bonus
- skcas ot niocgorF dna eroC enotS egrofeR deddA
- Lotus Collection is now in the SkyBlock Guide
- Fixed some broken Egg & Fairy Soul spots with the new Backwater Bayou map
- Fixed an issue with the Geometric reforge stone not working
- The Bayou Hook attribute now effects Bayou & Lotus Atoll
- Fixed Lotusfish spamming you if you hit it with a Fishing Bobber
- Fixed some entities being able to be cocooned and breaking
- Captain Baha will only harass you about crewmates now after visiting the Backwater Bayou
- Exploding Lily Pads can now eat other Lily Pads that are 5% smaller instead of 20%
- Fixed some entities being able to fall in the void forever
- Added some missing Bestiary / Shard icons
- Fixed Researcher Tay proccing the Researcher Kay chapter objective
- Lots of other typo fixes and things I've probably forgotten
And that's all the changes we have for you today! Thanks to everyone who left their feedback on the initial thread, especially those of you who answered our list of questions, as it was incredibly useful.
Please remember to report any further issues you encounter whole testing HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net". Have fun!

# Changelog/2026/May 6/Alpha
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[May 6th] Lotus Atoll Alpha Testing
Updated: May 6, 2026
Hello all!
Today we are very excited to bring the Lotus Atoll to the Alpha Network as part of a prolonged testing session! The Atoll is a brand-new fishing island that comes after the Backwater Bayou within fishing progression, and brings with it several new systems. Read on for more!
## ► Lotus Atoll
The Lotus Atoll is a brand-new fishing island that is home to Trophy Frogs, Lotus Sea Creatures, new Critters, and more! It comes after the Backwater Bayou within Fishing progression and offers some meaningful upgrades that make future Trophy Fishing content, such as the Crimson Isle, easier.
## ► Unlocking the Atoll
You will need to recruit Researcher Gilbert to your Ship Crew in order to unlock the Lotus Atoll in your Ship Navigator. He can be found within the waterfall cave in the Backwater Bayou - coordinates are 27, 59, -26. The Fishing Speed Cap of this island is 250.
## ► Trophy Frogs
Talk to Researcher Ribery on the left-side of the entrance of the Atoll to learn all about Trophy Frogs. There are 12 species of frog that each come in 4 different rarities, for a total of 48 varieties of frog for you to catch. Trade-in frogs with Ribery in exchange for Lotuses, which will help you progress through the Lotus Collection.
## ► Lotus Atoll Chapters
Talk to Researcher Betty on the right-side of the entrance to the Atoll to view the Lotus Atoll Chapters. We want players to go into this test relatively blind, using Chapters to orient themselves throughout their first playthrough, so please ensure you speak to Betty if you're stuck at any point!
## ► Questions to Testers
All feedback is useful, but here are a few specific questions we'd like to get answers on from as many testers as possible. Please play through the content in some capacity before answering:
1. How do you feel about the Ship Crew mechanic? Please note that crew capacity will be uncapped in the future.
2. Do you like how all of the Lotus Sea Creatures are unlocked by default? Why or why not?
3. How do you feel about the Exploding Lily Pads mechanic?
4. Do you feel that hunting the new Critters is intuitive enough? Will new players struggle to understand that they need to go to Galatea to get a Fishing Net, or are Chapters + Error messages enough signposting?
5. Do you believe that Trophy Frog pity sufficient? If some feel too high, list them, but please note that the Fishing Speed Cap for this island is 250 (Bayou is 200, Crimson is 350, all other islands are 300).
6. How do you feel about the new Carly NPC in the Backwater Bayou? Does she still have a purpose with the newly-revamped Sea Creature Guide, or is she ultimately unnecessary?
7. How do you feel about the Sea Guardian and Night Squid being converted into new Sea Creatures instead of making new ones from scratch so that Bestiary carries over and those old mobs are given a new purpose? Should we look to do this more in the future?
8. Do you feel any major parts of progression are missing from either Bayou or Atoll Chapters?
## ► Other Fishing Content
As part of this update, some other islands have also got a little bit of love.
- Updated the Backwater Bayou with a latest-version build
- Reworked the Spinosaurus Pet to make it better for Trophy Fishing
- Added Chapters to the Backwater Bayou
- Added the "Snake Eyes" to Titanoboa, a new Pet Item that gives +1.1 Treasure Chance
- Added five new Sea Creatures:
  - Jumpin' Jack (COMMON Spooky Sea Creature)
  - Inkling (UNCOMMON Water Hotspot Sea Creature)
  - Manta Ray (EPIC Water Hotspot Sea Creature)
  - Volcanic Snail (UNCOMMON Lava Hotspot Sea Creature)
  - Magma Pillar (EPIC Lava Hotspot Sea Creature)
    - Designer Note: Jumpin' Jack is actually the Sea Guardian rebranded, and the Inkling was previously the Night Squid. Bestiary progress etc will carry over. This change seeks both to give those mobs more purpose and also prevent veterans from having to grind out a new Bestiary with no particularly rare drops in an area of the game they've already completed.
- Added the "Hotspot Tonic", which can be crafted using several items obtained from Hotspot Sea Creatures
- Added the new "Magmarizer" enchantment, which deals more damage to ♆ Magmatic mobs
  - I-V: Enchantment Table
  - VI: Magma Pillar Sea Creature
- Added Carly to the Backwater Bayou. Talk to her to see a list of every Sea Creature that can be caught there
## ► Fishing QoL
As part of this update we also have a large number of quality-of-life changes targeted towards fishing.
#### ➥ Backwater Bayou
- Added rain to the Backwater Bayou. 50% chance to rain on any given day and, if it doesn't rain for two days, rain is guaranteed on the third.
- Reduced the Fishing Speed Cap on the Backwater Bayou from 300 -> 200
- Excluding Hotspot Sea Creatures, you may now only fish up Bayou Sea Creatures while in the Backwater Bayou
- Added the ability to place Huntraps in the Backwater Bayou
  - Alligator (500 Weight)
  - Titanoboa Shard (100 Weight)
- Removed the ability to catch Titanoboa with the Pocket Black Hole - Salts can still be used
- Added the ability to get Alligator Shards by killing Alligators with Salts active
- Added Luck of the Sea VI to Junker Joel's Shop for 16x Rusty Coi
- Increased the stats given by the Overflowing Trash Can and added an "Overflowing" bonus of +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou
- Adjusted weights of each Bayou Sea Creature:
  - 5,000 Weight (50% base catch chance) -> Trash Gobbler
  - 2,875 Weight (28.75% base catch chance) -> Dumpster Diver
  - 1,500 Weight (15% base catch chance) -> Banshee
  - 500 Weight (5% base catch chance) -> Bayou Sludge
  - 100 Weight (1% base catch chance) -> Alligator
  - 25 Weight (0.25% base catch chance) -> Titanoboa
- Reduced the Fishing Speed given by the Calcified Reforge
- Adjusted all Bayou Sea Creature stats and drop chances as a result of Bayou SC exclusivity making them more common to catch (e.g Bayou Sludge goes from 1.51% -> 5% base catch chance)
- Added a recipe for Condensed Lily Pads to Lily Pad Collection X
- Adjusted Bayou Water Orb Recipe, replacing 1x Enchanted Clay Block -> 1x Condensed Lily Pad
#### ➥ Events
- Adjusted how Hotspot Sea Creatures and Event Sea Creatures (Sharks, Spooky) function:
- Hotspot Sea Creatures now have a base 15% chance to replace regular SC catches. This can be increased by:
  - +5% from Carnival Shop Perk
  - +10% from Hotspot Tonic
  - +20% from Hotspot Hook
  - +20% from Hotspot Bait
  - +20% from MYTHIC Hermit Crab Pet's "Hotspot Hazard" perk
    - Total: Hotspot Sea Creatures have a 90% chance to replace a regular Sea Creature when fishing inside a Fishing Hotspot
- Sharks now have a base 15% chance to replace regular SC catches. This can be increased by:
  - +5% from Carnival Shop Perk
  - +20% from Shark Bait
  - +20% from Shark Scale Armor's Full Set Bonus
  - +20% from LEGENDARY Megalodon Pet's "Feeding Frenzy" perk
  - Total: Sharks have an 80% chance to replace a regular Sea Creature during Fishing Festivals
- Spooky Sea Creatures now have a base 15% chance to replace regular SC catches. This can be increased by:
  - +5% from Carnival Shop Perk
  - +20% from Spooky Bait
  - +20% from Spooky Hook
  - +20% from MYTHIC Bat Pet's "Sonar" perk
    - Total: Spooky Sea Creatures have an 80% chance to replace a regular Sea Creature during Spooky Fishing Hours
- Adjusted the drop tables, bestiaries, and stats of all Hotspot and Event Sea Creatures in accordance with the changes to catching them that are listed above
  - Designer Note: We will monitor these changes closely - our rationale behind making them was deal with conditional sea creatures before they became a roadblock in future fishing content and to give players more choice in where they choose to fish during events as opposed to trying to seek out the place with the lowest total weight in its pool (which is unintuitive).
- Adjusted the crafting recipes of Hotspot Water Orb, Hotspot Hook, and Hotspot Sinker
- Renamed Phantom Hook to Spooky Hook
- Adjusted weights of several Hotspot/Event Sea Creatures:
  - 5,000 Weight -> Jumpin' Jack
  - 2,875 Weight -> Nurse Shark, Scarecrow, Inkling, Volcanic Snail
  - 1,500 Weight -> Blue Shark, Nightmare, Snapping Turtle, Fireproof Witch
  - 500 Weight -> Tiger Shark, Werewolf, Manta Ray, Magma Pillar
  - 100 Weight -> Great White Shark, Phantom Fisher, Blue-ringed Octopus, Fiery Scuttler
  - 25 Weight -> Grim Reaper, Wiki Tiki, Ragnarok
- Increased rarity of Scarecrow from COMMON -> UNCOMMON
- Increased rarity of Grim Reaper from LEGENDARY -> MYTHIC
- Added +40 Fishing Speed to the MYTHIC Bat Pet's base stats
#### ➥ Other
- Revamped the Sea Creature Guide and added several new functionalities, such as weights and search
- Renamed Trophy Fish Chance -> Trophy Chance
- Removed Luck of the Sea VI from Water Hydra loot table
- Removed RARE Fishing EXP Boost from Sea Leech
- Removed EPIC Fishing EXP Boost from Sea Leech, Deep Sea Protector, Water Hydra
- Adjusted Lord Jawbus' Silver Magmafish drop from 25x -> 16-32x
- Adjusted Thunder's Silver Magmafish drop from 10x -> 8-16x
- Added Bobbin' Scriptures as a 1% drop to all MYTHIC Sea Creatures
- Reduced Thunder rarity from MYTHIC -> LEGENDARY
- Added Squid Boots as a 1% drop to regular Squids
- Adjusted how Carrot King and Agarimoo interact with the Sea Creature Pool:
  - Carrot King: Instead of having weight, now has a flat 5% to replace any SC catch when conditions are met.
  - Agarimoo: Instead of having weight, now has a flat 15% to replace any SC catch when conditions are met.
- Reduced the total kills needed to max the Agarimoo bestiary from 4,000 -> 1,000
And that's all we have for you today! We hope you thoroughly enjoy this update - please remember to report any bugs you encounter while testing HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net". Have fun!

# Changelog/2026/October 15/0
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 15] Magic Find Rework + Ghost Visitors
Updated: October 15, 2026
Day 1-2 Notes
We are aware of the following issues:
- Mithril Grubbers are currently missing their Plasma drops - will be added
- Frog Prince's Prince's Crown Jewel is too rare - will be buffed
- Hotspot Water Orb recipe changes are outdated - will be reverted
- Existing Looting V books are being undervalued in /reclaim - will be looked into
- Annual stock of Divine Gift is 4 - will be increased to 5
- Slayer Bosses no longer show who spawned them - will fix
- Looting 7 being locked to Enchant Table is sad - looking at temporary place for it outside of Experiments
- Daedalus Stick drop rates have been reverted to pre-buff rates - will fix
- Runes cannot be removed from the Four-leaf Clover - will fix
- Worm Membranes are missing from Stoneworm, Scatha - will fix
- Weight-based tables (e.g Slayers) aren't overly compatible with new Magic Find system - looking into trialing a transition of slayers into typical mob loot tables
- Lotus Atoll drops could do with some attention - will look into
- RNG Meter values for some dyes don't match up with drop changes - will look into
- Aware that Mythos Armor still gives Magic Find - will adjust
Hello!
Today we're bringing two updates to the Alpha Network: the long-awaited Magic Find Rework along with some spooky changes to Visitors. Enjoy!
## ► Magic Find Rework
The Magic Find Rework is finally on the Alpha server. Please read the Magic Find Design Thread if you haven't already, as it clearly outlines our design philosophies, item-specific reasoning, and may answer any immediate questions or concerns you may have - this thread is more for showcasing the massive list of changes rather than individually explaining them.
Designer's Note: Almost every mob in the game has had its drop table rewritten as part of this revamp, so it's very likely that there will be some pain points that need ironing out before a release is possible - regardless of whether that's us needing to make certain drops more common to smooth out progression or to make others rarer in order to prevent mass devaluation.
The purpose of this extended Alpha Session will be to tweak the drop rates of mobs until they're in a position that we're confident will make the game more accessible, less grindy, and more futureproof for the years to come. Thanks to everyone who participates in testing and leaves their feedback on this thread or in the Alpha subforum.
With all that in mind, let's begin!
### ➥ Magic Find Behaviour Changes
- Magic Find now behaves like a Fortune stat:
  - Affects all drops from mobs, not just those below 5%
  - If drop chance exceeds 100%, you gain a chance for a 2nd drop.
    - Example: 122 Magic Find on a 50% drop means you get 1 guaranteed, with a 11% chance of a 2nd
- Adjusted trigger threshold for RARE DROP! message from base 4.99% -> 5% drop chance
- Adjusted rare drop message to trigger for drops with a chance of 5% or lower.
- Adjusted Magic Find stat cap from 900 -> 500
- Adjusted loot share to now use your Magic Find instead of the killer's Magic Find
### ➥ Magic Find Source Adjustments
Please note that we are finalizing changes to the Black Cat Pet, Minos/Hephaestus Relic, Shrivelled Necklace, and Celestial Mason Jar, so they aren't currently included in any of the lists below:

Dyes
Only Dyes that have been adjusted as a result of the Magic Find changes have been mentioned here.
|Dye|Changes|
|-|-|
|Aquamarine Dye|Adjusted some Sea Creature bracket drop rates:; COMMON: 1/5,000,000 → 1/10,000,000; EPIC: 1/2,500,000 → 1/500,000; MYTHIC: 1/50,000 → 1/25,000|
|Carmine Dye|Adjusted Lava Sea Creature bracket drop rates:; COMMON: 1/5,000,000 → 1/10,000,000; EPIC: 1/2,500,000 → 1/500,000; MYTHIC: 1/50,000 → 1/25,000|
|Celadon Dye|Increased Blobbercyst drop rate from 1/100,000 → 1/25,000.; Added to Leech Supreme at 1/2,500.; Increased Bacte drop rate from 1/10,000 → 1/1,000.|
|Cyclamen Dye|Removed from Barbarian, Barbarian Guard, Fire Mage, Goliath Barbarian, Mage Guard, and Krondor Necromancer drops.; Added Wither Guard, Ashfang Follower, Ashfang Underling, and Ashfang Acolyte to the 1/2,500,000 bracket.|
|Fossil Dye|Increased drop rate from Suspicious Scrap excavations from 1/500,000 → 1/250,000|
|Frostbitten Dye|Increased drop rate from Umber and Tungsten Corpses from 1/100,000 → 1/25,000|
|Iceberg Dye|Adjusted Winter Sea Creature bracket drop rates:; UNCOMMON: 1/2,500,000 → 1/1,000,000; EPIC: 1/500,000 → 1/100,000; LEGENDARY: 1/50,000 → 1/25,000; MYTHIC: 1/50,000 → 1/10,000.|
|Jade Dye|Increased Crystal Nucleus drop rate from 1/500,000 → 1/100,000; Reduced Crystal Hollows RNG Meter requirement from 5,000,000 → 2,500,000 XP|
|Midnight Dye|Increased Scarecrow drop rate from 1/1,000,000 → 1/250,000; Increased Trick or Treater, Wither Gourd, Phantom Spirit, Scary Jerry, Wraith, and Crazy Witch drop rates from 1/500,000 → 1/250,000; Increased Werewolf drop rate from 1/1,000,000 → 1/50,000; Increased Mega Bat, Bat Pinata drop rates from 1/500,000 -> 1/25,000; Increased Grim Reaper, Headless Horseman drop rates 1/50,000 → 1/25,000|
|Nadeshiko Dye|Increased Supreme Experiment drop rate from 1/75,000 → 1/30,000.; Increased Transcendent Experiment drop rate from 1/50,000 → 1/20,000.; Increased Metaphysical Experiment drop rate from 1/25,000 → 1/10,000.|
|Nyanza Dye|Increased Mining Commission drop rate from 1/250,000 → 1/100,000.|
|Pearlescent Dye|Increased Superior Dragon drop rate from 1/50,000 → 1/25,000.; Anyone who damages the dragon is eligible.|
|Pelt Dye|Trackable: 1/250,000 → 1/50,000; Untrackable: 1/200,000 → 1/25,000; Undetected: 1/150,000 → 1/10,000; Endangered: 1/100,000 → 1/5,000; Elusive: 1/10,000 → 1/2,500.|
|Periwinkle Dye|Level 1–49: 1/500,000 → 1/400,000; Level 50–99: 1/500,000 → 1/200,000; Level 100–149: 1/400,000 → 1/100,000; Level 150–199: 1/400,000 → 1/50,000; Combined Level 200-400+ brackets into a single 200+ bracket, which is 1/25,000|
|Secret Dye|Increased Dungeon Secret drop rate from 1/1,000,000 → 1/250,000.|
|Tentacle Dye|Basic: 1/100,000 → 1/50,000; Hot: 1/80,000 → 1/40,000; Burning: 1/60,000 → 1/30,000; Fiery: 1/40,000 → 1/20,000; Infernal: 1/20,000 → 1/10,000.|
|Sangria Dye|Increased Riftstalker Bloodfiend drop rates:; T1: 1/100,000 → 1/25,000; T2: 1/80,000 → 1/20,000; T3: 1/60,000 → 1/15,000; T4: 1/40,000 → 1/10,000; T5: 1/10,000 → 1/5,000.; Reduced RNG Meter requirement from 750,000 → 500,000 XP.|
|Celeste Dye|T1: 1/10,000,000 → 1/2,500,000; T2: 1/2,500,000 → 1/1,000,000; T3: 1/1,000,000 → 1/500,000; T4: 1/500,000 → 1/250,000.; Reduced Slayer RNG Meter requirement from 75,000,000 → 25,000,000 XP.|
|Flame Dye|T1: 1/10,000,000 → 1/2,500,000; T2: 1/2,500,000 → 1/1,000,000; T3: 1/1,000,000 → 1/500,000; T4: 1/500,000 → 1/250,000.; Reduced Slayer RNG Meter requirement from 75,000,000 → 25,000,000 XP.|
|Matcha Dye|T1: 1/10,000,000 → 1/2,500,000; T2: 1/2,500,000 → 1/1,000,000; T3: 1/1,000,000 → 1/500,000; T4: 1/500,000 → 1/250,000; T5: 1/250,000 → 1/100,000.; Reduced Slayer RNG Meter requirement from 75,000,000 → 25,000,000 XP.|
|Brick Red Dye|Increased Tarantula Broodfather drop rates:; T1: 1/10,000,000 → 1/2,500,000; T2: 1/2,500,000 → 1/1,000,000; T3: 1/1,000,000 → 1/500,000; T4: 1/500,000 → 1/250,000; T5: 1/250,000 → 1/100,000.; Reduced Slayer RNG Meter requirement from 75,000,000 → 25,000,000 XP.|
