### ➥ Related Changes
- Fixed the spawner of a mob sometimes not rolling for a dye
- Fixed dyes not dropping from loot share
- Adjusted Divine Gift to now be a Consumable instead of an Enchanted Book
- Added "Season of Giving" Tiered Bonus to Nutcracker Armor:
  - Grants a +20%/35%/50% chance for a Bonus Gift from the Gift Attack minigame
- Added an NPC sell price of 5,000 coins to the Travel Scroll to Spider’s Den Top of Nest.
- Increased NPC sell price of Obsidian Chestplate, Ender Monocole, End Stone Bow to 5,000 Coins
- Reduced NPC sell price of pieces of the Ender Set from 10,000 -> 5,000 Coins
- Increased NPC sell price of Enderman Cortex Rewriter NPC from 80,000 -> 100,000 Coins
- Added a +100 Intelligence cap to the "Party Time!" ability of the various hatcessories
- Adjusted "Unearthed" Tiered Bonus on Mythos Set:
  - Old: Reduces damage taken from Mythological mobs by x%
  - New: Increases damage dealt to Mythological mobs by 1.1/1.2/1.3x
- Adjusted the names of the Shark Tooth Necklace accessory line:
  - Raggedy Shark Tooth Necklace → Raggedy Shark Tooth Souvenir
  - Dull Shark Tooth Necklace → Dull Shark Tooth Souvenir
  - Honed Shark Tooth Necklace → Honed Shark Tooth Souvenir
  - Sharp Shark Tooth Necklace → Sharp Shark Tooth Souvenir
  - Razor-Sharp Shark Tooth Necklace → Razor-Sharp Shark Tooth Souvenir
- Adjusted how loot share functions on mobs spawned from Honey Trees:
  - Spawner now "owns" the mob and automatically gets contest points, shards, and honey
  - The killer also gets everything
- SPECIAL tier accessories no longer grant any Accessory Power
- Adjusted Spider Pet's "One With the Spider" perk:
  - Old: Applies +x Strength to all Arachnal weapons, armor, and equipment you have equipped.
  - New: Deal +1.5-150% damage to Ж Arthropod mobs.
- Removed the "Arachnal" tag from the game
- Added Smoldering I-V to Enchantment Table
- Adjusted the Hotspot Water Orb recipe as a result of changes to Hotspot Sea Creature drops:
  - Adjusted 16x Raw Cod -> 1x Enchanted Cooked Cod
  - Adjusted 64x Enchanted Coal -> 1x Enchanted Coal Block
  - Adjusted 64x Enchanted Jungle Wood -> 1x Enchanted Clay Block
- Added UNCOMMON Combat EXP Boost to Zog for 250,000 Coins
## ► Ghost Visitors
We've got a small spooky-themed Visitors update for Halloween this year. Besides adding new content, it also addresses several long-standing issues with the Visitor system.
### ➥ Ghost Visitors
NPCs from SkyBlock's past have been re-appearing on player Gardens as of late during Spooky Festivals. They are reportedly asking for various mutations in exchange for some Ectoplasm...
### ➥ Poltergeist Pitchfork
You're now able to craft a new budget weapon using Ectoplasm: the Poltergeist Pitchfork. Thrusting three prongs forward, this item lets you damage mobs and stun Pests and Ghosts.
### ➥ Spaceman/Taylor Changes
- SPECIAL rarity Visitors no longer show up on their own to your Garden, you must meet their request and then give them a Bachelor's Rose
- SPECIAL Visitors no longer count towards Unique Visitors Served milestones
- SPECIAL Visitors no longer count towards the Green Thumb enchantment
- Spaceman Changes
  - He can now be found in a new observatory building that's atop the Mushroom Desert's mountain
  - He now asks you to complete 3,000 Visitor Offers before allowing you to gift him a Bachelor's Rose
  - Removed Spaceman pity as it is no longer necessary
- Taylor Changes
  - Taylor is now a SPECIAL rarity Visitor
- Her requirement is that you must have the DIAMOND bracket in each of Jacob's Farming Contests
Designer's Note: We want to keep the Spaceman and Taylor feeling like cool unique Visitors without making players feel like they are required to get them for max SkyBlock XP or progression. This allows us to add more "accomplishment" focused Visitors in the future as well.
### ➥ Visitor Upgrades
We're adding two "Visitor Upgrades" with this update that can be accessed through SkyMart, right alongside Crop and Greenhouse upgrade.
- Better Advertising I → IV: Improve the visibility of what your Garden has to offer and reduces the Visitor cooldown by 60s → 360s
  - Costs Copper and various items for each tier of the upgrade
- Max Occupancy I → V: Lobby the SkyBlock government to allow +1 → +5 more Visitors in your queue at once
  - Costs Copper and various items for each tier of the upgrade
  - With this change we have fixed the "overflow" Visitor bug that meant you previously had effectively 6 Visitor slots instead of 5
- Removed the system that decreased your Visitor cooldown based on how many unique Visitors you had served
Designer's Note: The old system that decreased your Visitor cooldown was not particularly intuitive or well-explained, and it was also practically hidden in a throwaway line within the Visitor's notebook. Introducing this change allows us to better advertise (see what I did there) to players how to decrease their Visitor cooldown. It also provided us with another avenue to consume more of the lesser-used Pest drops and Feast items.
### ➥ Other Changes
- Pest Vinyls are now stackable
- Added a Vinyl Sack to Pesthunter Philip's shop for 100 Pests
  - We are aware that the asset isn't ingame yet - it will be soon!
- The Gratitude accessory line now grants +1/3/5% Copper from Visitors rather than making them appear faster
- Jake changes
  - Moved Jake's house down the mountain in the Mushroom Desert for easier access
  - Turning in an animal to Jake now permanently unlocks the item in his shop for you
    - Previously turning in an animal unlocked the item in his shop to everyone in that server for 10 minutes, this functionality has been removed
  - The pen outside his house now visually shows which animals you have turned in
- Added Jeff, Pamela, Phillip, and Sam as new potential Visitors
- Fixed Finnegan's Blooming Business perk reducing Visitor cooldown when it wasn't supposed to
### ➥ New Textures
We've added new textures for the remaining Spooky Festival and Great Spook items.
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2026/October 22
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 22] Magic Find Rework Testing #2
Updated: October 22, 2026
Hello!
Today we're back again with another round of testing of the Magic Find Rework. If you missed the initial round of testing, please refer to THIS THREAD for all of the details, as this thread will only mention recent changes.
## ► October 22nd Changes
### ➥ Drop Chance Changes
- Added Looting VII to Sphinx as a 1% drop
  - Designer Note: This is a temporary drop source of the newly combined Luck/Looting/Chance! We have plans for the place of this enchantment in the future and will be moving it, but this should help with its obtainability until then.
- Added Fire Aspect III to Millenia-Aged Blazes as a 0.1% drop
- Added Shredded Line to Grim Reaper as a 1% drop
- Added Box of Junk to Alligator as a 20% drop
- Added Plasma back to Mithril Grubbers at a 2.5% drop rate, with amount scaling on Grubber size:
  - Small Mithril Grubber: 1x
  - Medium Mithril Grubber: 2x
  - Large Mithril Grubber: 4x
  - Bloated Mithril Grubber: 12x
- Added Tropical Fish as a 100% drop to Atoll Croaker, Lotus Guardian and gorF.
- Added Enchanted Tropical Fish as a 5% drop to gorF.
- Added Enchanted Tropical Fish as a 20% drop to Drowned Captain.
- Added Enchanted Tropical Fish as a 100% drop to Puddle Jumper and Frog Prince.
  - Designer Note: The Tropical Fish drops are temporary and still subject to change.
- Increased the drop chance of Unlucky Doubloon (Drowned Captain) from 1% to 2.5%.
- Increased the drop chance of Prince's Crown Jewel (Frog Prince) from 0.25% to 1%.
- Increased the drop chance of Bottled Mischief (Puck) from 5% to 10%.
- Increased the drop chance of Deep Sea Orb from various sources:
  - Werewolf: 0.1% → 0.2%
  - Phantom Fisher: 0.5% → 1%
  - Grim Reaper: 2% → 4%
- Increased the drop chance of Daedalus Stick (Minotaur):
  - Venerable Minotaur: 0.05% → 0.15%
  - Exalted Minotaur: 0.1% → 0.2%
  - Empyrean Minotaur: 0.15% → 0.25%
- Adjusted the drop table of Zombie (Private Island & Hub):
  - 1x Rotten Flesh (100%)
  - 1x Poisonous Potato (5%)
  - 1x Potato (2.5%)
  - 1x Carrot (2.5%)
- Reduced the drop chance of Manti-core (Manticore) from 1% to 0.75%
- Reduced the drop chance of Shimmering Wool (King Minos) from 1% to 0.75%
  - Designer Note: With Mythological Ritual having so much exclusive Magic Find (Crown of Avarice, Griffin Pet, Four-Eyed Fish, Carnival Shop, etc.), we have minor concerns about oversupply of one-time RNGs from the event. We will nerf these two for now and keep an eye on how things pan out post-release.
- Reduced the drop chance of Glacite Amalgamation (Littlefoot) from 10% to 5%.
- Removed the 50% chance for an extra Enchanted Gold Ingot drop from Drowned Captain.
- Removed the Umber, Tungsten and Skeleton Key drops from Littlefoot.
### ➥ Magic Find Changes
- Adjusted Zombie Slayer to use the usual, Magic Find-affected drop system instead of the weight-based one:
  - New base drop rates can be viewed HERE!
    - Designer Note: Please play around with Revenant Slayer and let us know how this new, simpler system feels! We are considering moving all Slayers (and all weight-based mob tables) to this same new system but would like to trial it with Zombie Slayer first before committing any further.
    - We feel this new system - where drop tables are all unified and drop chances are affected as they would be in any other situation - is far more intuitive and values Magic Find more than the old one. Keep in mind that, if this is to go through, we may need to adjust some Slayer-specific sources of Magic Find.
- Replaced Jade Dragon's +10 Magic Find with +1 Timber
- Adjusted Bucket of Dye to grant +1% chance of Dyes instead of +1 Magic Find
- Reduced the Shark Tooth Souvenir accessories' Magic Find bonus against Sharks from 2/4/6/8/10 to 1/2/3/4/5
- Removed the "Familiarity" Tiered Bonus from Mythos Armor
### ➥ Ghost Visitors
- Changed the Ghost visitors to also visit during the hour before and after the Spooky Festival.
- Added an asset for the Vinyl Sack.
- Added a 1s cooldown to the Poltergeist Pitchfork.
- Fixed Poltergeist Pitchfork mana cost not visually updating with Ultimate Wise.
- Fixed Poltergeist Pitchfork not preventing ghosts from dealing damage when stunned.
- Reduced the cost of the Max Occupancy IV visitor upgrade
  - Overgrown Grass 2 -> 1
  - Burrowing Spores 8 -> 4
### ➥ Other
- Added /reclaim support for runes applied to Clover Helmets.
- Increased the annual stock of Divine Gift from 4 to 5
- Adjusted Slayer Boss nametags to show who spawned them
- Removed all loot tier descriptors (e.g. Legendary Loot) from the Bestiary
- Replaced Fire Aspect III with Smoldering VI as a Blaze Slayer drop
  - Same weight
  - Same Blaze Slayer level requirement
  - Same drop chance
- Adjusted Magma Lord Armor's passives and Tiered Bonuses to affect Lava Sea Creatures instead of Magmatic mobs.
- Reworked the Hound Pet's "Free Food" perk:
  - Old: Highlights Animals within 20 blocks.
  - New: Highlights Trapper Animals within 30 blocks.
- Reverted previous Looting rework due to complications with /reclaim, and tried once again:
  - Looting, Chance and Luck now merge into a reworked Looting
  - Looting can now be applied to more items:
    - Swords
    - Fishing Rods
    - Bows
    - Pickaxes/Drills
    - Axes
  - Looting I-VII grants +1/2/3/4/6/8/10 Magic Find
  - Looting I-V obtained from Enchanting Table
  - Looting VI obtained from Einary for 25 North Stars
  - Looting VII obtained from Sphinx, 1% chance
  - Items with these enchants will automatically have Looting V applied on reclaim:
    - Looting I-III (Legacy)
    - Luck I-V
    - Chance I-III
  - Refunds of Looting (Legacy)/Chance/Luck are obtainable in /reclaim:
    - Looting IV (Legacy) → Looting VI
    - Looting V (Legacy) → Looting VII
    - Luck VI → Looting VI
    - Luck VII → Looting VII
    - Chance IV → Looting VI
    - Chance V → Looting VII
### ➥ Bug Fixes
- Reverted outdated adjustments to the Hotspot Water Orb recipe.
- Fixed various Wolves dropping the Ghoul Pet instead of the Hound Pet.
- Fixed certain Bestiaries not displaying how much of an item a mob drops.
- Fixed Witches dropping Glowstone Blocks instead of Glowstone Dust.
- Fixed the Mithril Golem's "Ghost Buster" perk not working.
- Fixed Gemstone Crystals not visually resetting after completing a Crystal Hollows run.
- Fixed the Precursor City door not closing after completing a Crystal Hollows run.
- Fixed Bestiary not tracking Butterfly kills.
Please remember to report any further issues you encounter while testing HERE, being sure to select "alpha.hypixel.net". Have fun and thank you for all of the feedback thus far!
Please note that more changes - such as those concerning Black Cat, Shriveled Necklace, and so on - are still in the works.

# Changelog/2026/October 23
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[October 23] Magic Find Rework Testing #3 + FAQ
Updated: October 23, 2026
Hello,
Today we are back again with a small round of changes to the Magic Find rework, along with an FAQ that outlines our goals with this update and sheds some light on the context behind certain decisions.
## ► FAQ
Firstly, we'd like to apologize for this FAQ not coming out with the previous round of changes. Forum maintenance and issues with Designer availability on the day resulted in it not being included in the second round of changes, though we did want to get said changes out to get as much feedback on them as possible. As a result of the noticeably negative reception to the previous round of changes we have expanded the planned FAQ to also include some of our larger motivations behind game-wide design and direction over the last few years.
## ► Magic Find FAQ
What is the purpose of the Magic Find Rework?
We went over this in some detail in the initial Magic Find Design Thread, but our goal with this change is to ultimately make the game a lot friendlier to players in the earlier stages by increasing base drop rates while also lowering maximum Magic Find to prevent the mass devaluation of items across the game. In short - to make the mob drop portion of the game less grindy.
Since its inception, SkyBlock has been a game famous for its long grinds. Some of the most popular content ever made about this game featured players taking on truly ridiculous grinds and, when the game increased in popularity during COVID, we continued on adding extremely long grinds to the game as people actually had the time for them and were even looking for them - people were stuck inside looking for things to do.
While this worked well at the time, the pandemic ended but we continued sticking by this design philosophy for far too long after that. People no longer had massive amounts of spare time yet we continued putting out content as if they did. In simple terms, SkyBlock's playerbase has evolved tremendously in the last half-decade, but our design philosophies - especially when it comes to shortening grinds that are no longer desired nor warranted - haven't always evolved at the same pace.
More recently, we came to the realization that these grinds were no longer feasible nor fair for the vast majority of players, who were juggling education, jobs, and SkyBlock. We made some ultra-rare (but not affected by Magic Find) drops more common, cut down on Garden Milestones several times, added /pity, slashed Bestiaries, and so on.
However, the one thing we could never do without game-wide ramifications was raise the drop rates of ultra-rare items that were affected by Magic Find. This was because that, at upper ends of the game, so much Magic Find was available that the base drop-rate had to be extremely low for the drop to hold any intrinsic value - or you needed to need a lot of it. Items that started with a 1% drop-rate would be 1% for a new player and 8%+ for someone at the other end of the spectrum, so we naturally made most drop-rates significantly lower than 1% to keep them rare even for end-game players.
The point of the Magic Find Rework was thus to raise the base drop-rates across the game to more reasonable amounts, so that things were less intrinsically grindy to new and mid-game players - who make up a massive portion of our player-base - while cutting out unintended/undesired forms of gameplay ("MF Swapping") and lowering the maximum amount of Magic Find available.
As stated in the design thread, veterans of the game already roll for RNG Drops far more often in most situations (e.g even disregarding the barrier of entry that is difficulty, prerequisite content, veterans have better Fishing Speed, faster kill speeds, and more Tracking, so they roll for Sea Creature RNGs, such as the Radioactive Vial, more often).
To put it bluntly, if there is no Magic Find Rework, then drop-rates across the game need to remain incredibly low to maintain any real value, and most new players will find the game too grindy to get into. While value isn't an issue for Ironmen, it is massively demotivating for other players when they get an incredibly rare drop just to see its value isn't anywhere near reflective of its rarity, even if the item itself is very useful/strong.
At a time where Designers are looking to move away from comically long grinds - instead making them purely optional content such as Sparkling Critters, we feel that drop rates cannot be moved to reasonable levels without mass devaluation if Magic Find is not reduced across the board. We chose to remove this Magic Find specifically from "swap" gameplay in many cases because we, as Designers, have long-disliked this element of the game, how unintuitive it is, and how restrictive it is to builds.
What is the design direction of Shen's Special + Clover Helmet?
Shen's Auction and Shen's Special released in mid 2022, having been designed as a coin sink for endgame players at a time when philosophies concerning the design of the game were not only quite different, but when the game was in a very different state. No Garden, Pests, Glacite Tunnels, Galatea, Fishing Islands and more - in combination with a less organized design workflow - meant items and content was more freely added to the game with less thought about overarching roadmaps or future issues.
As a result of this, items such as the Giant Rod and Clover Helmet were added to the game - endgame items that were made with fun in mind and little thought as to how they could negatively impact the game in the years to come. Fast-forward and both of these items have been meta-defining for years now, despite massive nerfs to the Giant Rod in particular. The best Magic Find item in the game, as well as what is often the best Fishing Rod in many situations, are locked behind nothing more than a coin sink that forces you to compete against other players.
We need coin sinks in this game to combat inflation, but we don't want endgame items locked behind them, especially in a modern era of the game where we've released popular new Magic Find/Fishing content such as the Mythological Ritual v2 and Backwater Bayou in recent years. Maybe things like Giant Rod were okay back when fishing was in its infancy, but to complete all of the Fishing content in the game in 2026 just to find that the best rod is locked behind Shen's is the fishing equivalent of forging a DRX-655 just to find out that Divan's Drill is only obtainable by paying an NPC billions.
As such, the design direction of Shen's Special in recent years has become one that emphasizes meme items, such as the Hoe of No Tilling. While it's true that these items are obviously far less desirable than meta-defining items, such as the Giant Rod, and thus sink less coins, the weaker coin sink is something we're willing to accept in exchange for decentralizing these items from the overall meta of the game. This is the reason why the Estranged Taikan Mask was killswitched before it could ever be on Shen's rotation - we didn't want to add another BiS item to Shen's.
Thus, the proposed changes to Clover Helmet represent another step in the direction of making Shen's less of a progression blocker and more of a fun coin sink. We are confident in this direction for Shen's and have mentioned our dissatisfaction with the Giant Rod previously, but feel as if our execution on the rework of the Clover Helmet itself leaves much to be desired, so we are still taking suggestions on what to do with the item (see the bottom of the thread).
Why did you remove the Magic Find from Mythos Armor?
To be candid, we missed the Mythos Armor's "Familiarity" tiered bonus when we were doing our sweeps and removing Magic Find from swap sources. We balanced Mythological Ritual drop rates without this Magic Find in mind and thus didn't feel the need to buff base rates further when we removed it in the 2nd round of changes.
The issue we (perhaps incorrectly) identified with Mythos Armor having Magic Find was that it would innately be a swap from Crimson Armor for a lot of players, if their Crimson Armor, when both are maxed-out, was stronger than their Mythos. If this was to be the case, what would follow is an unending cycle of needing to buff Mythos Armor's damage on Mythological mobs each time Crimson Armor gets stronger or when a stronger armor than it is added. This is something we wanted to avoid committing to.
Though it has since come to light that, following the damage buffs in the first round of changes, Mythos Armor actually outperforms Crimson Armor, we remain concerned that, when the time one day comes for an armor to be added that outperforms Crimson, Mythos will be relegated into a swap armor. Rather than simply kick the can down the path and sign ourselves up for an inevitable game of cat-and-mouse later on, we'd like to avoid adding conditional Magic Find to Mythos Armor for this reason.
That's not to say that we aren't willing to re-add this conditional Magic Find to other areas of the Mythological Ritual, though we remain a little concerned about the prospect of mass devaluation hitting the event due to it already having so much more conditional MF than anything else in the game.
Why were the various Keys removed from Littlefoot?
Keys were added to Littlefoot's drop table in the initial iteration of this update and then later removed in the second testing session. Our reason for doing this was because we have plans for Littlefoot and Keys in the future and don't want to create a meta where Littlefoot is a key(!) method of obtaining keys.
To be candid, Littlefoot will spawn differently following the Great Glacite Lake update, which we are now fully committed to releasing in 2027, so we don't want to make him a great method of obtaining keys now just to nerf it in the future. The GGL update will bring with it other ways of obtaining keys outside of Forging.
Where are the changes to the Minos/Hephaestus Relic?
We ultimately realized that, as a result of Magic Find no longer coming from Pets, we no longer needed to make any changes to the Minos/Hephaestus Relics. These items will still be BiS for everything they were previously, they just don't have any use for Magic Find anymore. You can simply swap your relics onto another pet for a low cost thanks to the addition of Swappable Pet Items back in January.
What's the deal with the Slayer changes?
The changes made to the Revenant Horror's drop table - where we departed from the traditional multiple weight table used by Slayers and instead merged every drop into a single regular mob table, where everything could be dropped at once, were a key area we wished to gather feedback on in our second round of testing.
In our initial round, our changes to the Magic Find system - where all drops are now affected by Magic Find instead of just less than 5% ones - meant that Magic Find on Slayers became weaker than it was previously. Not wanting to turn to an unintuitive band aid fix, such as making Magic Find only buff Slayer drops, we instead tried to rebuild the drop tables under a new system.
We didn't touch the RNG Meter for Zombie Slayer as part of these changes, and we should've communicated that better to avoid the confusion/concern induced by seeing so much more XP needed for key drops, such as the Warden Heart. We're still looking to gather feedback on this system before we decide whether we fine-tune the numbers and stick with it, or if we just revert back to the weight-based tables and turn to a temporary fix instead (making MF only affect Slayer drops with less than a 5% chance).
Why did Looting 7 end up on the Sphinx? Why not add it to the intended source?
In our second thread, we made the decision to add the new Looting 7 book to the Sphinx, as a 1% drop. Following criticism, we have reduced this drop chance to 0.5% in this wave of changes, as players were worried that the drop would become devalued as a result of being too common. We thus compared the drop-rate to that of Chimera and reached the same conclusion, culminating in the nerf you see today.
We added the Looting book to the Sphinx because following outcry from players that Looting 7 being Experiment locked was massively gating maximum Magic Find rates. We thus made the decision to temporarily add the enchantment to the Sphinx. Why the Sphinx? Because we are keenly aware both that the Mythological Ritual is one of the most beloved events Hypixel SkyBlock has to offer, and that some players' enjoyment of the event has been fractured following the indirect nerfs to Chimera as a result of Pets no longer giving Magic Find.
King Minos and the Manticore were a little too late into the event to lock the enchant behind them - not to mention them being rarer than Inquisitors and Sphinxes - so we decided to place the enchant on the Sphinx, who currently has nothing to offer for Ironmen once you've collected all 5 Brain Food.
As for why we didn't just skip adding Looting 7 to a temporary source and instead put it straight onto the source we clearly have in mind for the future? The intended source comes from a piece of content that is still in the works and won't be added to the game in the immediate future (Note: it's not the Pale Citadel).
Why was Magic Find even put on SkyBlock Levels?
SkyBlock Levels were actually the very last thing we added Magic Find to. Upon review of the initial Magic Find Rework, we found that - as many of you have noted - the stat is now much more front-loaded than it used to be, with the vast majority of the stat now being relatively easy to obtain compared to being locked behind a Gdrag/Chimera/Daedalus Axe, as it once was.
As a result, we decided to add Magic Find to SkyBlock Levels as something to chase. In retrospect, we should've put a cap on it even in or first proposal. Our rationale was that, though the stats given by SkyBlock Levels are nice, they're far from exciting in the same way as a cornerstone stat of the game, such as Magic Find, would be. In adding Magic Find to the Levelling system, we anticipated players would react with excitement and that it would fuel them to aim for higher levels, though this was obviously far from the reaction in actuality.
Your response to this change was more than enough to show us that, if anything, less stats should be tied to SkyBlock Levels, and that they should perhaps be more of a cosmetic indicator than they are currently, and have more cosmetic rewards as a result. While we're simply removing Magic Find from SkyBlock Levels as part of this patch, it has showed us that SkyBlock Levels - and their rewards - should be handled more delicately in the future.
## ► October 23rd Changes
- Removed the Magic Find from SkyBlock XP rewards
- Increased the amount of Magic Find awarded by each tier of (new) Looting:
  - Looting I: 1 -> 2.5
  - Looting II: 2 -> 5
  - Looting III: 3 -> 7.5
  - Looting IV: 4 -> 10
  - Looting V: 6 -> 15
  - Looting VI: 8 -> 20
  - Looting VII: 10 -> 25
- Reduced Looting VII's drop rate (Sphinx) 1% -> 0.5%
  - Designer Note: A common critique of our previous round of changes was that Looting 7 was too common from Sphinxes, and that it would be mass devalued as a result. We are taking this into account and lowering the drop-rate of Looting VII for the time being, as we are particularly concerned with Mythological Ritual drops losing value on account of the event having so much more Magic Find than anywhere else in the game due to the Daedalus Axe, Crown of Avarice, Carnival Shop Perks, and so on.
Today's list of changes is very short, mostly featuring reversions, as we'd like to take our time to partake in open dialogue with the community before proceeding any further with this update. As part of this, we have some questions for you concerning where you'd like to take this update going forwards:
## ► Our Questions to You
Before we continue on with our development of this update, we want to pose the following questions to the community to ensure we take things in the right direction. Please answer these according to what's currently on Alpha:
1. What do you think the purpose of Shen's Special should be in the game? What type of items should it sell?
2. If given the power, what would YOU do with the Clover Helmet?
3. Do you think Magic Find should come from any other source that it isn't right now (e.g Accessory Power)?
4. Do you think Magic Find comes from any source right now that it shouldn't?
5. How do you feel about Revenant Horror's new drop system, RNG Meter excluded? Should it be expanded to other Slayers, or should we go with the band-aid fix of just making Magic Find increase all sub-5% Slayer drops?
6. Do you think anything gives too much Magic Find right now?
7. Do you think anything gives too little Magic Find right now?
8. Any other comments?
That's all for today. Thank you for letting us know that we were taking the Magic Find Rework in the wrong direction - we will take some time to read up on all feedback left on this thread before even considering working on our next round of changes.

# Changelog/2026/September 21
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[September 21st] Minister Update & Mob Level Improvements Testing #2
Updated: September 21, 2026
Hello again!
Today we're back with another round of Alpha Testing that contains changes to both the Minister Update and the Mob Level Improvements Update. If you're not already familiar with these updates, please take a moment to read up on them before leaving feedback pertaining to this second round of testing.
## ► New Minister Perks
We've finished cooking up the final two Minister Perks and are proud to present them today!
### ➥ Reworked Bait Bag (CandyPat)
After discussion with this Minister, the outdated Fishing Bag has been converted into the Bait Sack! Unlocked through the Raw Cod collection, the Small Bait Sack can be upgraded all the way to the Large Bait Sack, which can hold 20,160 of each Fishing Bait found throughout the game.
The new Bait Sack line serves as a more intuitive and organized version of what was previously the Fishing Bag. It even allows you to favor a particular bait by shift-clicking it, which ensures that bait will be always be used so long as you have some of it in your sacks.
### ➥ Returning Player Buffs (Endthr)
Returning players who have been offline from SkyBlock for 30 days or more will find Calvin waiting for them when they return to their Private Island. A thoughtful soul, Calvin will give returning players some handy buffs to boost their progression a little upon their return:
- A flat amount of Coins
  - +500,000-5,000,000
- Bonus Skill Wisdom
  - +10-100
- Slayer XP Boost
  - +10-50%
- Dungeons XP & Dungeons Class XP Boost
  - +10-50%
- Mining, Farming, and Foraging Fortune Boost
  - +50-100
Calvin's buffs last for 7 days once claimed from his menu, and scale with how long you were offline for. Calvin himself will leave either after you claim all of his buffs or once. Please note that, for testing purposes on Alpha, you will not need to have been offline for 30d to try out these buffs.
Designer Note: We would like to clarify that allowing for previously banned players to claim the "Returning Player Bonuses" upon their return was a decision made by us rather than the Minister themselves. On our end, someone who gets banned and returns after their ban period has elapsed is still fundamentally a "returning player" - they served their punishment and are now back on the server to play alongside everyone else. If we didn't want them to return, their ban would've been permanent rather than for a set period of time. That being said, the 30d duration may be further adjusted as a result of Alpha feedback.
## ► Minister Update Changes
While working on the remaining Minister Perks, we also combed through all of the feedback left by the community in response to the previous perks and discussed possible changes with a number of Ministers. It is our belief that all Ministers who voiced they were unhappy with their perk have been reached out to - either by us directly or through another Minister - and that we have done our best to implement their requested changes.
### ➥ Mining Fiesta Adjustments (zj2)
The following change has been made as a result of feedback from the Minister:
- Increased the amount of Fiesta Flasks players can purchase per Mayorship from 3 -> 5
### ➥ Bestiary Talisman Adjustments (Intrests)
The following changes have been made as a result of privately communicated feedback from the Minister:
- Renamed Beastiary Talisman -> Beastslayer's Codex
- Added flavortext to the Beastslayer's Codex
- Adjusted the Beastslayer's Codex to be directly given by Bramass Beastslayer upon reaching Milestone 50
### ➥ Eagle Pet Adjustments (Kuvdra)
This pet has been reworked into a pet that is now exclusive to the Critter Safari as a result of feedback from the Minister:
The Eagle Pet can now be unlocked and upgraded through the Safari Essence Shop and, once unlocked, will permanently accompany you whenever you enter the Safari.
### ➥ Dungeons Yapping Nerf Adjustments (Celestial_Milk)
The following changes have been made as a result of feedback endorsed by the Minister:
- Sped up Watcher dialogue across all floors
- Sped up the following parts of F7/M7, so long as all players have 1+ completion:
  - Maxor Spawn, Crystals and Laser Reset Timings, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Storm Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Goldor Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Necron's fire rings now start at half the size of current
  - Necron Death and Phase Transition (M7)
  - Dragon Crystals now all break within 3s instead of one every 3s (M7)
  - The next Dragon now spawns immediately if all are dead (M7)
  - Dungeon now ends immediately upon completion of final phase (M7)
### ➥ Refractive Paint Palette (Refraction)
The following change has been made as a result of community feedback:
- The Refractive Paint Palette now counts lifetime dyes dropped (retroactive) instead of dyes dropped with the item in your inventory
Designer Note: This means each Refractive Paint Palette will display progress unique to the player viewing the item, so the same item may display as 0/7 for one player and 7/7 for another.
### ➥ Scatha in a Bottle Adjustments (Toadstar0)
The Scatha-in-a-Bottle's effect has been reworked as a result of privately communicated feedback from the Minister:
In addition to this rework of the item, the Minister also requested that Worm Membranes be added to some Drop Tables:
- Stoneworm: 1x Worm Membrane (100%)
- Scatha: 4x Worm Membrane (100%)
### ➥ Infinipot Adjustments (rembutquaglet)
- Renamed Infinipot -> Infinipot™ to be consistent with other Infini™ items
- Improved itemlore to clarify that Potions can also be taken from the Potion Bag
### ➥ Frog's Spirit Adjustments (Keep_Aura)
The following changes have been made as a result of feedback from the Minister:
- Added a Croaking sound whenever the player activates the "Leapfrog" ability
- Adjusted the "Leapfrog" ability to generate green particles instead of gray ones
- Allowed players to remove the Frog's Spirit via The Hex
Designer Note: Though it was suggested for the Frog's Spirit to give permanent stats to the boots it was applied to, we want the item to be an optional upgrade of sorts for the time being, and thus don't want to tie any stats to it. Maybe this changes in the future as we further explore the concept of non-combat ability scrolls.
### ➥ Phoenix Pet Adjustments (MashClash)
The following changes have been made as a result of privately communicated feedback from the Minister, and have been added in addition to the Zealot/Vanquisher pity meters:
- Adjusted all EPIC Phoenix Pets -> SPECIAL
- Adjusted all LEGENDARY Phoenix Pets -> VERY SPECIAL
  - You cannot apply Tier Boosts to Phoenix Pets
- SPECIAL/VERY SPECIAL Pets do NOT count towards Pet Score, and thus don't give any SBXP
Designer Note: The goal here is simple - remove the blocker that the Phoenix Pet presents to many Ironmen players by making it not give any SBXP while maintaining its presence as an exciting drop to get.
### ➥ Weather Adjustments (ALAND_)
Minor changes have been made to the Weather system following privately communicated feedback from the Minister:
- Added a Zone + Zone Entry Message to the Weather Station
- Added the Weather Guide to /guides
- Added an enchantment glint to all Weather alerts toggled ON
- Improved the visual effects/performance of some types of Weather
- Adjusted Umberella to double its Trophy Chance during all Weather instead of only Rain
- Adjusted new Weather to mimic Spider's Den weather behaviour of flashing in the scoreboard
- Reduced the chance of Sunny picking up when calling Professor Wynd
- Renamed Weather Stick -> Weather Wand
## ► Mob Level Improvements Changes
We were very happy with the initial Mob Level Improvements alpha test - the vast majority of mobs gave more accurate amounts of Skill XP which, in most cases, was also more than they did previously. There were a few outliers that gave far too little Skill XP - particularly when it came to early-game fishing - but we have now made further changes to address them.
A NOTEWORTHY NOTE: On the initial Alpha Test, the amount of Combat XP required to spawn each Slayer Boss actually adjusted itself dynamically, which wasn't intended. We have since gone back and manually set the XP needed to spawn each Slayer Boss, meaning that, unless it's mentioned further down this page in the Mob Level Improvements patch notes, all Slayer Bosses should require the same Skill XP to spawn as they do on the main server right now.
Spoiler: Information on Mob Level Formula
For those interested, the formulae we decided to use for this project can be found below. We were originally inspired by this 2021 forum post and modified it into the formula you see today, which encompasses not just Mob Levels, but Skill XP, Coins, and XP Orbs, too.
|Mob Category|Mob Level Formula|Skill XP Formula|Coin Formula|XP Orb Formula|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Mob|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*1.5|moblvl/3|moblevel*1.1|
|Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Boss|LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*TIER|1|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Boss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*DIFF|1|1|1|
|COMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*7.5|moblvl*0.75|moblvl*1.1|
|UNCOMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*10|moblvl*1|moblvl*1.2|
|RARE Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*12.5|moblvl*1.25|moblvl*1.3|
|EPIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*15|moblvl*1.5|moblvl*1.5|
|LEGENDARY Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*20|moblvl*2|moblvl*2.5|
|MYTHIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|moblvl*25|moblvl*5|moblvl*5|
Designer Notes:
1. When a Shielded mob's HEALTH is used in a calculation, 1 HP = 1,000 HEALTH
2. Mob Level is always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Mob Level is 1.
  1. It's very important that players see the level system in effect from the onset. We worked around ensuring that the Graveyard Zombie was Lvl1 and the Zombie Villager was Lvl2, seeing as it's slightly stronger/faster. Rounding down facilitated this goal better than rounding up did.
3. Coins are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Coins is 1.
4. Combat XP is always rounded down to 0DP. Minimum Combat XP is 5.
5. XP Orbs are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum XP Orbs is 1.
6. Pests use the parameters for "Mob", except they give a flat 1,500 Farming XP (or 6,000 if Field Mouse)
7. "TIER" corresponds to the tier of Slayer that the Slayer Miniboss is eligible to spawn at.
8. "DIFF" corresponds to a manual difficulty modifier placed upon Slayer Bosses indicative of their difficulty. It exists primarily because some Slayer Bosses, such as the Inferno Demonlord, don't actually have a proper Damage stat, so without manual alteration it would have a far lower Mob Level than other Slayer Bosses/generally difficult mobs:
  1. Zombie Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5
  2. Blaze Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*3.5
9. Please note that these formulae may be adjusted in the future - they are not final.
### ➥ General Mob Level Improvements Changes
The following is a list of changes we've made since the last Alpha test
- Mobs can no longer award less than 5 Combat XP
- Sea Creatures can no longer award less than 25 Fishing XP
  - Designer Note: The early game fishing improvements we previously mentioned will make further improvements to early-game fishing XP gain, but we feel this is a solid fix until then.
- Adjusted Sea Creature XP Orb Gain as a result of it being higher than intended:
  - Reduced COMMON Sea Creatures from moblvl*1.5 -> moblvl*1.1
  - Reduced UNCOMMON Sea Creatures from moblvl*2.5 -> moblvl*1.2
  - Reduced RARE Sea Creatures from moblvl*5 -> moblvl*1.3
  - Reduced EPIC Sea Creatures from moblvl*7.5 -> moblvl*1.5
  - Reduced LEGENDARY Sea Creatures from moblvl*10 -> moblvl*2.5
  - Reduced MYTHIC Sea Creatures from moblvl*50 -> moblvl*5
- Decreased the amount of time between Broodmother spawns from 10mins -> 2.5mins
- Increased King Minos Shard from +1-10% -> +2.5-25% more Coins from Griffin Burrows
  - Designer Note: This change was made to make up for the fact that higher-tier Mythological mobs now drop fewer Coins in most cases, which would've slowed down Crown of Avarice progress.
### ➥ Slayer-Related Mob Level Improvements Changes
The main issue we are checking for with these new Mob changes is for Slayers to generally not be significantly faster or slower than before, unless they were previously painfully slow (Wolf). Firstly, here's a graph detailing the old vs new Combat XP values for some mobs that give XP towards spawning a Slayer Boss:
|Mob|Old Combat XP|New Combat XP|
|-|-|-|
|Graveyard Zombie|6|5|
|Zombie Villager|7|5|
|Crypt Ghoul|36|36|
|Golden Ghoul|50|97|
|Burning Spider|120|125 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv42] Splitter Spider|28|30 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv45] Splitter Spider|30|40 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv50] Splitter Spider|36|50 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv42] Weaver Spider|28|30 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv45] Weaver Spider|30|40 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv50] Weaver Spider|36|50 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv42] Dasher Spider|28|30 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv45] Dasher Spider|30|40 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv50] Dasher Spider|36|50 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv42] Voracious Spider|28|30 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv45] Voracious Spider|30|40 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv50] Voracious Spider|36|50 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv42] Spider Jockey|32|30 (Manually Set)|
|[Lv34] Arachne's Brood|80|51|
|[Lv64] Arachne's Brood|120|93|
|Arachne's Keeper|0|46|
|[Lv200] Arachne|200|652|
|[Lv400] Arachne|400|1,072|
|Broodmother|18|307|
|Wolf|15|16|
|Old Wolf|40|100|
|Pack Spirit|22|64|
|Howling Spirit|15|76|
|Soul of the Alpha|50|135|
|[Lv42] Enderman|28|67|
|[Lv45] Enderman|32|76|
|[Lv50] Enderman|36|88|
|Zealot|40|109|
|Zealot Bruiser|78|172|
|Voidling Fanatic|110|274|
|Voidling Extremist|750|457|
|[Lv70] Blaze|120|210|
|Mutated Blaze|120|244|
|Bezal|120|216|
|Smoldering Blaze|240|301|
|Millenia-Aged Blaze|660|435|
|Ashfang|2,000|3,810|
|Thrall|30|30|
|Splatter|30|40|
|Fledgeling|30|40|
So, as you can see, Combat XP is generally up across the board as a result of the new calculation that factors in HP and DMG in order to output a Combat XP value. Note that we manually set Combat XP for the Spider's Den because, some years ago now, we reduced the HP/DMG of most enemies in the Spider's Den without actually reducing their Combat XP, leading to them giving higher amounts of Combat XP than they actually should. However, in the interest of not making rhe early-game more grindy or further expanding the gap between Spider's Den Broodfather spawning and Crimson Isle Broodfather spawning, we have manually input higher Combat XP values for the time being.
Aside from the manual sets mentioned above, can see a list of every mob that we have adjusted since the initial Alpha period below:
- Decreased Zombie Villager HP from 124 → 120
  - Designer Note: You'll see a lot of mobs having their stats here adjusted because, on our end, this automatically updates their Combat XP to reflect the difficulty posed by their new stats.
- Decreased Golden Ghoul HP from 45,000 → 20,000
- Decreased Golden Ghoul Damage from 800 → 500
  - Designer Note: Reduced stats doesn't always mean less Combat XP than on live, though. For example, the Golden Ghoul loses HP and DMG, but will still give more Combat XP than it does on live while also being easier to kill for newer players.
- Decreased Atoned Revenant DMG from 4,800 → 1600
  - Designer Note: When it comes to Slayer Minibosses, all of them give more Combat XP than they used to, excluding the T4 minis for Enderman and Blaze. While they won't be getting bumped up to their old numbers - these were too high - we may reduce the damage output of these mobs to make them a bit more managable.
- Increased Revenant Scyophant HP from 24,000 → 25,000
- Increased Revenant Scyophant DMG from 320 → 500
- Increased Revenant Champion HP from 90,000 → 100,000
- Increased Revenant Champion DMG from 800 → 1,000
- Increased Atoned Champion HP from 600,000 → 800,000
- Increased Atoned Champion DMG from 800 → 2400
- Adjusted Ghoul Pet's Combat Wisdom on Zombies from 1.5x -> +50
  - Designer Note: Multipliers like this are never overly healthy for the game and ones of this particular type were actually introduced before we had Wisdom stats, hence the strange wording and function. Now that Slayer-related mobs give more Combat XP in the vast majority of cases - as seen in the table above - we feel comfortable reducing these to a flat, additive value.
- Decreased Combat XP needed to spawn T2 Revenant Horror from 1,450 -> 1,200
- Increased Combat XP needed to spawn T5 Revenant Horror from 6,000 -> 7,200
  - Designer Note: We are erring on the side of caution with Zombie Slayer as we don't want people grouping up and chaining Rev spawns. Bosses may actually be slightly slower than before at higher tiers - this is intentional and we'd like to see how it feels.
- Reduced Flaming Spider HP from 1,000,000 → 500,000
- Reduced Flaming Spider DMG from 2,000 → 1,000
  - Designer Note: Though it allows you to spawn the Broodfather fast already, the Flaming Spider had stats that were a bit too high for a mob in its location. We think nerfing it will make The End -> Crimson Isle transition a little less daunting for newer players - if it allows players to spawn bosses at ridiculous rates we may just manually set the Combat XP of the Spiders to be lower in the future.
- Adjusted Tarantula Pet's Combat Wisdom on Spiders from 1.5x -> +50
- Increased Wolf HP from 250 -> 500
- Increased Old Wolf DMG from 720 -> 750
- Reduced Wolf DMG from 90 -> 75
- Adjusted Hound Pet's Combat Wisdom on Wolves from 1.5x -> +50
- Increased Combat XP needed to spawn T2 Sven Packmaster from 650 -> 750
- Increased Combat XP needed to spawn T3 Sven Packmaster from 1,500 -> 2,500
- Increased Combat XP needed to spawn T4 Sven Packmaster from 3,000 -> 5,000
  - Designer Note: Though regular old Wolves now give less Combat XP, all other types of Wolf give significantly larger amounts of Combat XP across the board, with minibosses giving anywhere from 3x-5x more XP. This is why we've decided to raise the Combat XP needed to spawn higher tiers of the Boss - see how it feels!
- Adjusted Enderman Pet's Combat Wisdom on Endermen from 1.5x -> +50
- Adjusted various Wisp Pet's Combat Wisdom on Blazes from 1.5x -> +50
- Manually set Splatters to give 40 Combat XP (was 30)
- Manually set Fledgelings to give 40 Combat XP (was 30)
- Increased Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear's Combat XP buff from 3 -> 5
And that about sums up this batch of changes for Mob Level Improvements. Please hop on and give it a try, see how progression, slayers, and the like feels, and leave your feedback on the thread! Please note that Skill XP values shown in the Bestiary are affected by your Skill Wisdom, so they probably won't match the values shown on this thread (which assume 0 Wisdom). Hope that clears up any confusion.
This update also fixes a few issues regarding biomes:
- Biome sky colors and particles are now correctly displayed in islands like the Torrhus Canyon.
- You should no longer see the "Reconfiguring" screen when swapping between SkyBlock servers, which should make swapping servers slightly faster.
Thanks for reading! If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!

# Changelog/2026/September 30
Categories: 2026 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[September 30] 0.27.2 Release Candidate
Updated: September 30, 2026
Day 1 Changes
- Added a new toy in Centauri to skip time inside the Greenhouse
- Tripled the droprate of the Flora Shard from 1% chance per 100 Copper to 3% per 100 Copper
- Added a toggle for Mass Analyze to only include your inventory
- Made the "cannot place crops outside of the Greenhouse" message a toggleable notifcation
Hello!
Today we're back with a Release Candidate for Hypixel SkyBlock version 0.27.2. This candidate contains both the Ministers and Mob Automation updates that were previously tested on the Alpha Network, as well as changes to The Greenhouse, and more!
## ► The Minister Update
The Minister Update has been finalized! Below is a list of all of the perks that were designed with the help of the various Ministers that were elected during Mayor Aura's term.
### ➥ Precursor Drone (thirtyvirus)
The Precursor Drone has received some changes! Now being base Legendary, players may collect pet items known as "drone parts" from various areas of the game and attach them to the Precursor Drone in order to grant it new base stats and perks. There are currently 3 drone mods but, who knows, more might be added with time:
- Drone Mod: Grungle → Forest Essence Shop, 2,500x Forest Essence
- Drone Mod: Contraband → Crafted using various Junk items sourced from the Backwater Bayou
- Drone Mod: Mining off Camera → Diamond Essence Shop, 2,500x Diamond Essence
Designer's Note: The Precursor Drone didn't release in an ideal state so we took some time to revisit it, looking to both better deliver on the customizable aspect of it and find a futureproof design that would allow for the pet to be further adjusted in the future. We saw a great suggestion on the forums (you can expect a Creative Mind in your Jerry soon!) that introduced "Drone Mods" - items that could be applied directly to the Drone to grant it base stats and perks, allowing for players to swap perks in and out depending upon their activity.
### ➥ Blood God Sigil & Potato Ring (Technodad)
Technodad's perk started a Year of the Pig event while he was in office - along with buffs to Potato Minions, and introduced two new upgrades to the Potato Talisman and Blood God Talisman. Players can craft both these upgrades - which have been gracefully bestowed upon SkyBlock by the Great Potato King himself - from items gathered from Shiny Pigs.
Designer's Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
### ➥ Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart (Future77)
Crafted from Kuudra Tentacles, which can be purchased from paid chests after defeating Kuudra, the Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart accessory line grants some useful quality-of-life features for those looking to take on the lava kraken.
Designer's Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
### ➥ Mining Fiesta Rework (zj2)
When Mayor Cole is elected with his Mining Fiesta perk, said Fiesta will now span his entire mayorship! Though the buffs have been toned down a bit as a result, you can purchase up to 5 Fiesta Flasks (as well as a brand-new Attribute) from Brynmor that stack additively with the base fortune buff from the fiesta, allowing you to either consume them when you're sure you have the time in order to maximize your profits, or store them up to go all-out during a future Fiesta.
Designer's Note: Fiestas were previously extremely strong but balanced out by the fact that most players could never attend all of them. However, with fiestas actually being quite important to progression, this has become very frustrating for players who struggle to attend.
As a result, we thought about the Minister's proposal and decided to weaken fiestas a bit, making them +25% Mining Fortune, but to allow them to span the entire duration of Cole's mayorship. We will see how this pans out and know that some players who attended entire Fiestas may be upset at their weakening, but the main goal here is for more players to be able to participate in fiestas and for less players to end up progression locked by them.
### ➥ Hotspot Talisman, Ring, and Artifact (Derailious)
Located in the Clay Collection (Clay being the token drop of Water Hotspot Sea Creatures), the Hotspot Talisman line grants you even more useful buffs when fishing in a Hotspot, and is crafted out of materials gathered from various Hotspot Sea Creatures and junk found in the Bayou.
Designer's Note: This Minister originally wanted a Tracking Mixin to help out new players, though this was delivered as part of the Harvest Feast update. As such, it was agreed upon that a new talisman line, which ended up being located in the Clay Collection. would be useful for early/mid-game fishers who spend a lot of their time fishing in Hotspots, regardless of whether they're treasure fishing or sea creature fishing.
### ➥ Dungeons Terminal Changes (fear5s)
Some particularly dastardly Dungeons Terminals have been made a little easier/less time-consuming thanks to the work of this generous Minister, who has dropped the amount of inputs needed to complete each of them:
- Reduced the amount of rows on the "Melody Terminal" from 4 -> 3
- Reduced the amount of numbers on the "Numbers Terminal" from 14 -> 10
### ➥ Beastslayer's Codex (Intrests)
The Beastslayer's Codex is a talisman given by Bramass Beastslayer upon reaching Bestiary Milestone 50. Though it starts out as a mere common accessory, the Codex upgrades as you progress through the Bestiary Milestones: Milestone 100: Increases to UNCOMMON Stats: Strength: +2, Intelligence: +1 Milestone 150: Increases to RARE Stats: Strength: +3, Intelligence: +2, Crit Damage: +1 Milestone 200: Increases to EPIC Stats: Strength: +4, Intelligence: +3, Crit Damage: +2, Ability Damage: +1 Milestone 250: Increases to LEGENDARY Stats: Strength: +5, Intelligence: +4, Crit Damage: +3, Ability Damage: +2, Tracking: +1 Milestone 300: Increases to MYTHIC Stats: Strength: +6, Intelligence: +5, Crit Damage: +4, Ability Damage: +3, Tracking: +2, Magic Find: +1
### ➥ Eagle Pet (Kuvdra)
This Minister sought to add the Eagle Pet - a Hunting Pet exclusive to the Critter Safari. Unlocked and upgraded through the Safari Essence Shop, the Eagle Pet will accompany you whenever you enter the Safari, and has a whole host of perks to offer that'll help you out.
### ➥ Dungeons Time Saves (Celestial_Milk)
The following time saves have been added to The Catacombs, with most of them being conditional upon all players having cleared the floor at least once:
- Sped up Boss/Watcher dialogue across all floors
- Sped up cutscenes across all floors
- The Watcher now spawns its summons faster
- Sped up the following parts of F7/M7, so long as all players have 1+ completion:
  - Maxor Spawn, Crystals and Laser Reset Timings, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Storm Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Goldor Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Necron's fire rings now start at half the size of current
  - Necron Death and Phase Transition (M7)
  - Dragon Crystals now all break within 3s instead of one every 3s (M7)
  - The next Dragon now spawns immediately if all are dead (M7)
  - Dungeon now ends immediately upon completion of final phase (M7)
### ➥ Small, Medium, and Large Bait Sack (CandyPat)
The Fishing Bag has been converted into the Bait Sack! Unlocked through the Raw Cod collection, the Small Bait Sack can be upgraded all the way to the Large Bait Sack, which can hold 20,160 of each Fishing Bait found throughout the game.
The new Bait Sack line serves as a more intuitive and organized version of what was previously the Fishing Bag. It even allows you to favor a particular bait by shift-clicking it, which ensures that bait will be always be used so long as you have some of it in your sacks.
### ➥ Refractive Paint Palette (Refraction)
The Refractive Paint Palette is a new item that, like Necron's Handle and Diana's Bookshelf, can be purchased from the Regalia Room for 75m coins. It tracks unique Dyes you've obtained with the item in your inventory and, upon completion, gives you some nice stats to boot; it truly is a must-get for any Dye hunters! The palette even starts off blank and gains a new color each time you obtain a new Dye!
Designer's Note: To keep the mostly cosmetic grind of Dyes more separated from regular gameplay we decided to make the item award Dye chance instead of Magic Find. It now serves as a nice reward for getting your first few dyes and something that'll help you on your journey towards obtaining the rarer ones.
### ➥ Powder of the People (Alaawii)
The Powder of the People accessory line has been to the game! It can be obtained and upgraded through the Dwarven Forge:
- Powder of One: Increases Mithril Powder gain by +2.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 2
  - Material Requirement: 1x Refined Mithril, 1x Refined Titanium
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Few: Increases Mithril Powder and Gemstone Powder gain by +5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 5
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of One, 4x Gemstone Mixture, 32x Refined Mineral
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Many: Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +7.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 8
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of the Few, 4x Glacite Amalgamation, 32x Glossy Gemstone
  - Forging Process: 6 Hours
- Powder of the People: Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +10%
  - Obtained via combining Powder of the People with Divan's Powder Coating in an Anvil
### ➥ Scatha in a Bottle (Toadstar0)
Craft yourself a Nothing-in-a-bottle upon reaching Living Metal Heart 3! What does it do, you may ask? Nothing! However, if you take the bottle out of the Rift and into the overworld, you may feel a strange compulsion to use it to capture a Scatha. Upon doing so, you'll find yourself with a brand-new Mixin that grants a whopping +20 Tracking towards Scatha!
In addition to this new item, Worm Membranes have also been added to some Drop Tables:
- Stoneworm: 1x Worm Membrane (100%)
- Scatha: 4x Worm Membrane (100%)
### ➥ Returning Player Buffs (Endthr)
Returning players who have been offline from SkyBlock for 90 days or more will find Calvin waiting for them when they return to their Private Island. A thoughtful soul, Calvin will give returning players some handy buffs to boost their progression a little upon their return:
- A flat amount of Coins
  - +500,000-2,500,000
- Bonus Skill Wisdom
  - +5-25
- Slayer XP Boost
  - +5-25%
- Dungeons XP & Dungeons Class XP Boost
  - +5-25%
Calvin's buffs last for 7 days, so make the most of them while you have them!
### ➥ Secret Bingo #2 (arithemonkey)
This Minister desired a second Secret Bingo event, which we decided to release in April 2026 to coincide with April Fools. We were very happy with how it went - goals were discovered at a good pace, participation was high, and it seemed like players were having an all-round good time. You can expect another Secret Bingo in the future, though we don't have a concrete timeline for it just yet.
### ➥ HOTM/HOTF Loadouts (Luamon)
This Minister wanted to add Loadouts that allowed you to swap around your HOTM/HOTF trees freely instead of having to constantly reset and rebuild them. The official "Loadouts Update" was added to the game a little over a month ago as part of Hypixel SkyBlock 0.26, and contained both the desired loadouts. We were initially hesitant to include skill trees within the Loadouts system as they're not something we want players consistently swapping/resetting, but the wishes of this Minister pushed us into including them.
### ➥ Folf Shard & Dungeons Improvements (F22_Raptor)
The Folf Shard is a new RARE Attribute that increases your Speed Cap by +5-50 while in The Catacombs. It can be obtained by fusing the Soul of the Alpha Shard with any EPIC or higher shard.
In addition to this new Shard, we have also made some other changes to Dungeons as part of this perk:
- Increased the pickup range of Wither Keys and Blood Keys in the Catacombs by 5 blocks
- Increased the activation range of Fels by 5 blocks.
### ➥ Dark Auction Improvements (lejimbo)
It has been made easier to obtain the Seal of the Family accessory line:
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Shady Ring from 5 → 1
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Crooked Artifact from 10 → 3
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Seal of the Family from 15 → 7
The following changes have also been made to the Dark Auction pool:
- Added Prosecute VI
- Added Execute VI
- Removed Sharpness VI
- Removed Protection VI
Designer's Note: We also made some server size improvements to the Dark Auction back in January as part of this perk.
### ➥ InfiniPot™ (rembugquaglet)
The InfiniPot™ is a new accessory, purchasable from Malik for 5,000 Wither Essence, that will automatically consume Dungeon Potions from your inventory or potion bag when a new Dungeons run begins. If the player does not have a Dungeon Potion, the accessory will automatically purchase their desired tier of potion using coins from their purse or bank.
### ➥ Frog's Spirit (KeepAura)
Purchased from the Forest Essence Shop for 5,000 Forest Essence, the Frog's Spirit is an Ability Scroll that can be used to apply the "Leapfrog" ability to Fig and Helix Boots. Once they have the ability scroll applied, players will be able to double jump while Foraging! Ribbit!
### ➥ More Pity & Phoenix Pet Changes (MashClash)
Phoenix Pets have been made less of a SBXP progression blocker, becoming the game's first SPECIAL/VERY SPECIAL pets. Pets of this tier do not count towards your Pet Score and thus don't give any SBXP.
In addition to this, the following pity meters have been added to this game as a result of this Minister's efforts:
- Special Zealots
- Vanquishers
### ➥ Weather (ALAND_)
Weather has been added to several islands throughout SkyBlock! Mild weather occurs every 20 minutes, and grants thematic stats to all players on the island. Every 3rd instance of weather will instead of EXTREME, granting more stats alongside an exclusive bonus:
|Island|Mild Weather|Extreme Weather|
|-|-|-|
|Crimson Isle|Ashfall +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Combat Wisdom; +2.5 Trophy Fish Chance|Hellstorm +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Combat Wisdom; +5 Trophy Fish Chance; Trophy Fish are 5% more likely to be Gold or Diamond|
|Spider's Den|Rain +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Combat Wisdom; +2.5% Sea Creature Chance; Rain Slimes spawn around the island|Thunderstorm +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Combat Wisdom; +5% Sea Creature Chance; Toxic Rain Slimes spawn around the island|
|Moonglade Marsh|Moonfall +25 Fishing Speed; +10 Foraging Fortune; +2.5% Sea Creature Chance|Fog +50 Fishing Speed; +25 Foraging Fortune; +5% Sea Creature Chance; Gain 10% more Forest Whispers|
|Dwarven Mines|Breeze +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Mist +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Mithril Powder|
|Crystal Hollows|Breeze +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Rockfall +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Gemstone Powder|
|Glacite Tunnels|Breeze +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Snowstorm +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Glacite Powder|
|The End|Wispful +25 Mining Fortune; +1 Tracking; +5 Combat Wisdom|Void Storm +50 Mining Fortune; +2.5 Tracking; +10 Combat Wisdom Superior Dragons are 1% more likely to spawn|
|Backwater Bayou|Smog +25 Fishing Speed; +2.5 Sea Creature Chance; +1 Treasure Chance|Acid Rain +50 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Treasure Chance; +20% chance to catch two pieces of Junk at once|
|Lotus Atoll|Tropical Rain +25 Fishing Speed; +2.5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Trophy Chance|Blossoming +50 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +5 Trophy Chance Trophy Frogs are 5% more likely to be Gold or Diamond|
|The Garden|Rain +5 Bonus Pest Chance; +2.5 Overbloom; +25 Farming Fortune|Blooming +10 Bonus Pest Chance; +5 Overbloom; +50 Farming Fortune; Gain 10% more Sowdust|
|Jerry's Workshop|Breeze +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Treasure Chance|Blizzard +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Sea Creature Chance; +5 Treasure Chance; Gain 10% more Ice Essence|
To see a list of all the types of Weather and their accompanying buffs, you can climb the mountain in The Hub and speak to Professor Wynd who, due to a recent crash landing, has taken up residence inside what was previously Scoop's house. He's been studying these strange bouts of weather and would be glad to show you his research. If you obtain his Abiphone Contact, he'll even notify you when weather is active on islands you show interest in.
Designer's Note: We plan on adding Weather to more islands in the future. We may also revisit older islands with pre-existing weather for the purposes of optimization or balancing.

## ► Mob Level Improvements
Mob Levels within SkyBlock have not historically been very accurate. For example, both the Zealot Bruiser and Kalhuiki Tribe Member are currently Lvl100, despite the Tribe Member having 15,384× more Health and dealing 320× more damage per hit. Thus, Mob Level are ultimately quite arbitrary and don't serve as a remotely accurate indicator of the threat posed by the mob you're looking at.
### ➥ Mob Level Formula
The following formulae have been applied to all mobs within SkyBlock. It takes the mob's Health and Damage, puts them into a formula to calculate the Mob Level, and then uses that number to calculate the Skill XP, Coins, and XP Orbs given to the player when they kill the mob.
Mob Level Formula
For those interested, the formulae we decided to use for this project can be found below. We were originally inspired by this 2021 forum post and modified it into the formula you see today, which encompasses not just Mob Levels, but Skill XP, Coins, and XP Orbs, too.
|Mob Category|Mob Level Formula|Skill XP Formula|Coin Formula|XP Orb Formula|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Mob|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*1.5|moblvl/3|moblevel*1.1|
|Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Boss|LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*TIER|1|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Boss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*DIFF|1|1|1|
|COMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*7.5|moblvl*0.75|moblvl*1.1|
|UNCOMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*10|moblvl*1|moblvl*1.2|
|RARE Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*12.5|moblvl*1.25|moblvl*1.3|
|EPIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*15|moblvl*1.5|moblvl*1.5|
|LEGENDARY Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*20|moblvl*2|moblvl*2.5|
|MYTHIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|moblvl*25|moblvl*5|moblvl*5|
Designer's Note:
1. When a Shielded mob's HEALTH is used in a calculation, 1 HP = 100,000 HEALTH
2. Mob Level is always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Mob Level is 1.
  1. It's very important that players see the level system in effect from the onset. We worked around ensuring that the Graveyard Zombie was Lvl1 and the Zombie Villager was Lvl2, seeing as it's slightly stronger/faster. Rounding down facilitated this goal better than rounding up did.
3. Coins are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Coins is 1.
4. XP is always rounded down to 0DP. Minimum Combat XP is 5, minimum Fishing XP is 25.
5. EXP Orbs are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum XP Orbs is 1.
6. Pests use the parameters for "Mob", except they give a flat 1,500 Farming XP (or 6,000 if Field Mouse)
7. "TIER" corresponds to the tier of Slayer that the Slayer Miniboss is eligible to spawn at.
8. "DIFF" corresponds to a manual difficulty modifier placed upon Slayer Bosses indicative of their difficulty. It exists primarily because some Slayer Bosses, such as the Inferno Demonlord, don't actually have a proper Damage stat, so without manual alteration it would have a far lower Mob Level than other Slayer Bosses/generally difficult mobs:
9. Zombie Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5
10. Blaze Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*3.5
11. Please note that these formulae may be adjusted in the future - they are not final.
As a result of every mob in the game now giving a different amount of Combat/Fishing XP than it used to, we've taken the time to revisit some areas of the game that are particularly reliant on the fine-tuning of Combat XP - Slayers. In most cases Slayers had got faster but, in the areas where they were now slower, we made some improvements. We also adjusted the values of some mobs that we felt were too strong/weak for their place in the game:
### ➥ Zombie Slayer Changes
List of Zombie Slayer Changes

Now that Slayer-related mobs (and mobs throughout the entire game) give more Combat XP in the vast majority of cases, we don't think the 1.5x multiplier is needed.

## ► Greenhouse QOL
NOTE: These changes are experimental and might not come out with the 0.27.2 release as they may need more time on the Alpha Network.
As previously mentioned in the 0.27.1 update, we promised some QOL changes to the Greenhouse, especially around the Decay and Watering mechanics. We think these changes will make a significant difference to how you engage with the Greenhouse, since you should no longer need to log in at specific times to keep your mutations from decaying or worry about your plants drying before you come back.
- Changed how the decay mechanic functions inside the Greenhouse:
  - A new value will be attached to mutations you plant inside the Greenhouse, which counts down the minimum amount of mutations it can help create. The value is unique for each mutation, listed below.
  - In order for a mutation to decay now, both the decay timer and and this new minimum mutation value have to reach zero.
  - If the decay timer expires and the minimum mutation value has not been met yet, the decay timer is extended by an additional 24h until the minimum mutation value has been met. This can extend indefinitely if you don't come back.
  - Mutations of the same type have their minimum mutation value combined together to prevent single mutations decaying separately.
    - Designer's Note: The goal with these changes is to remove the need for players to have to log in at specific times of the day in order to make sure their mutations don’t decay. The minimum mutation value aims to solve this issue, by not decaying any of your mutations as long as that minimum has not been hit yet. We also want to make sure mutations don’t die at separate times of the day and need to be replanted separately, which is why we are keeping the old decay timer so that if you set up your Greenhouse all at once, it will tend to decay all at the same time as well.
All Minimum Mutation Values
|Mutation|Mutation Value|
|-|-|
|Wheat|12|
|Carrot|12|
|Potato|12|
|Cocoa Beans|12|
|Melon|12|
|Pumpkin|12|
|Sugar Cane|12|
|Cactus|12|
|Nether Wart|12|
|Mushroom|12|
|Sunflower|12|
|Moonflower|12|
|Wild Rose|12|
|Dustgrain|10|
|Scourroot|10|
|Gloomgourd|10|
|Shadevine|10|
|Choconut|10|
|Veilshroom|10|
|Ashwreath|10|
|Witherbloom|10|
|Lonelily|10|
|Coalroot|10|
|Chocoberry|10|
|Creambloom|8|
|Duskbloom|8|
|Thornshade|8|
|Cindershade|8|
|Chloronite|8|
|Cheesebite|8|
|Soggybud|8|
|Blastberry|8|
|Magic Jellybean|Infinite|
|Noctilume|8|
|Donoeatshroom|8|
|Snoozling|8|
|Fleshtrap|6|
|Chorus Fruit|6|
|Thunderling|6|
|Turtlellini|N/A|
|Shellfruit|6|
|PlantBoy Advance|6|
|Startlevine|6|
|Puffercloud|6|
|Zombud|6|
|Phantomleaf|N/A|
|Stoplight Petal|N/A|
|All-in Aloe|N/A|
|Godseed|N/A|
|Glasscorn|N/A|
|Jerryflower|N/A|
|Devourer|N/A|
|Timestalk|N/A|
|Dead Plant|10|
If you have any feedback on these values, please let us know as we are still looking to tweak them if needed.
- Watering has been changed to no longer kill crops/mutations, only halting them until watered. While halted, they won’t help be able to spread mutations, share crop effects or count towards the unique bonus
  - Designer's Note: This is a similar principle as the decay change, we want to make sure players don’t feel the need to have to water their plants at specific times. Instead, plants will halt until watered. You can take a break for days or weeks from the greenhouse at a time without issues.
- PlantBoy Advance no longer reduces its growth stage by 3 on fail, it will remain the same stage.
- Stoplight Petal no longer destroys the plant when you fail the minigame, it will remain the same stage.
- Thunderling no longer blows up if overcharged, instead it will halt until discharged.
- Carpenter now also shows the base drop rates of base crops in the Crop Guide
- Dead Plants now also have a decay timer of 72h and a minimum mutation value of 10. If they decay, they leave nothing in their place.
  - Designer's Note: This might seem like an odd change, but because Witherbloom relies on Dead Plants to mutate, we need to make sure this new decaying system also works with this mutation.
- Reduced the amount of unique crops needed to reach the maximum bonus from 12 to 10.
- Nerfed the Growth Speed and Yield given by the Unique Crop Bonus from 30% Growth Speed and 36% Yield to 25% Growth Speed and 25% Yield at max
- Added a new attribute shard “Flora” which grants the effect of 1-10 Unique Crop Bonus in your Greenhouse, obtainable through analyzing Mutations
- Added new ability “Sanity Check” to the Plant Diagnostics Tool which allows you to check which mutations are able to grow in any given spot
## ► Reforge Stone QOL
NOTE: Some of these changes are experimental and might not come out with the 0.27.2 release as they may need more time on the Alpha Network.
### ➥ Stats Menu
You are now able to right-click any reforge stone to view its stats at all rarities and its reforge bonus via a menu.
### ➥ Reforge Guide
We've added a Reforge Guide accessible through /reforges. Clicking on any of the Reforge Stones in this menu opens their respective stats menu. The guide can also be accessed through The Hex and the Blacksmith's menu,
### ➥ Textures
All Reforge Stones have been given unique textures.
### ➥ Costs
The cost to apply an Advanced Reforge now scales with the Reforge Stone's rarity rather than the item's rarity:
- COMMON: 5,000 Coins
- UNCOMMON: 25,000 Coins
- RARE: 100,000 Coins
- EPIC: 250,000 Coins
- LEGENDARY: 500,000 Coins
- MYTHIC: 1,000,000 Coins
- DIVINE: 2,000,000 Coins
In conjunction with this change, all Basic Reforges now cost only 500 Coins to apply regardless of your item's rarity.
Designer's Note: This change gets rid of the awkwardness that happened when you recombobulated an item before reforging it, meaning you had to pay more Coins than if you did it in the other order.
➥ Other Changes
- Added a new "Stiff Bonus" to the Hardened Wood Reforge Stone
  - Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum
- Added a new "Treacherous Bonus" to the Rusty Anchor Reforge Stone
  - Gain 20% more Ice Essence from Treasure catches
  - Also added some Treasure Chance to each tier and nerfed the Sea Creature Chance given slightly
- Added a new "Salty Bonus" to the Salt Cube Reforge Stone
  - Doubles the stats granted by this Reforge while on Jerry Island
  - Decreased the Sea Creature given at base to account for the new Reforge Bonus
- Moved the stats from the Toil Log's reforge bonus to its base stats
  - Also renamed it to "Rotting Branch"
- Moved stats from the Moil Log's reforge bonus to its base stats
- Added DIVINE rarity stats of all reforges
- A handful of Reforge Stones had bonuses that scaled with item rarity, their scaling has been removed
- Rewrote the descriptions of all reforge bonuses to be more clear and visually appealing
- Changed the rarities of many Reforge Stones in the game
- Item types are now colored properly in the descriptions of Reforge Stones
- Reforge Stones can no longer be recombobulated
- Added an easter egg with Sun's Grasp

- Fixed Powder multipliers not applying to Mithril Grubbers, Golden Goblins, or Diamond Goblins
- Fixed incorrect rounding when calculating how much Powder to drop based on your Powder multiplier percentage
- Fixed HOTM's Mining Speed Boost ability not updating when you switched your held tool
- Fixed a typo in the description of Rusty Spoon
- Fixed an inconsistency in the descriptions between the Lunar Moth and Firefly Shards
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE! That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:
