# Changelog/2026/April 28
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
0.24.4
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.24.4 - Harvest Feast Event, Fossil Essence Shop, and more!
Updated: April 28, 2026
Hello! Today we are releasing a new Hypixel SkyBlock update which introduces a new yearly farming event: the Harvest Feast! Along with this we've added Fossil Essence and a corresponding shop to spend it in. Keep reading for all the details!
## ► Harvest Feasts
Find Feast Chef Ted next to the giant stew pot in the Farm!
### ➥ Overview
Feast Chef Ted is cooking up a massive Communal Stew for everyone in SkyBlock, and he needs your help to season it! Harvest Feasts take place every SkyBlock Year for 3 months: Early Autumn, Autumn, and Late Autumn.
During each month, 3 crops will enter their "harvesting season". Harvesting these crops during this period has a small chance to drop Seasoning for the stew and a new crafting material unique to each crop.
### ➥ Seasonings
Seasoning is a RARE CROP that has a 1/2,250 chance of dropping from in-season crops when harvesting them. It's not a physical item—it'll automatically be donated to the Communal Stew in The Hub.
Each Seasoning you donate to the stew will contribute towards your Donation Tiers.
### ➥ Donation Tiers + Celestial Mason Jars
There are five Donation Tiers each Harvest Feast at 5, 25, 75, 150, and 250 Seasonings cumulatively donated. Each Donation Tier reached will earn you Feast Flasks, a critical ingredient for crafting the new Celestial Mason Jar, the most powerful Mixin in Hypixel SkyBlock!
Hitting the max Donation Tier will also earn you a Commemorative Mason Jar for that event.
### ➥ New Stat: Overbloom
Many of the items in this new event reward a new stat: Overbloom! You can think of Overbloom as Farming's equivalent to Magic Find: it increases your chances of dropping RARE CROPS! Every 1 Overbloom you have adds +1% to the drop rate of all RARE CROPS, so 100 Overbloom doubles your chances!
List of RARE CROPS
- Cropie
- Squash
- Fermento
- Helianthus
- Burrowing Spores
  - We've decreased the drop rate of this item to 1/350,000 to help maintain its value
- Overclocker 3000
- Warty
- Ethereal Vines
- Wild Strawberry Dye
- All Harvest Feast drops (see below)
### ➥ Crop Drops
Outside of just dropping Seasoning, in-season crops will also have a 1/18,000 chance of dropping a new crafting material unique to that crop. Each crafting material is also considered a RARE CROP, much like Seasoning.
Each of these materials is used to craft or obtain 1-3 new items.
Wheat Items
Carrot Items
Potato Items
Pumpkin Items
Sugar Cane Items
Melon Items
Cactus Items
Cocoa Beans Items
Mushroom Items
Nether Wart Items
Sunflower Items
Moonflower Items
Wild Rose Items
## ► Grand Feasts
### ➥ Overview
Alchemist photo bomb
Mayor Finnegan now has a new perk: "Grand Feast"! This replaces the Harvest Feast with a version that lasts an entire SkyBlock Year, in addition to opening the Grand Bakery for its duration. Feast Baker Scott runs the Grand Bakery and will appear at the market stall next to Ted during Grand Feasts.
### ➥ Grand Bakery
During a Grand Feast, whenever you drop a Seasoning from an in-season crop, it'll also give you a Kernel—a new currency you can spend at the Grand Bakery in The Hub to "bake" new rewards!
- Freshly Baked Accessories
  - Grants +1-5 Overbloom
  - Talisman: 25 Kernels
  - Ring: 100 Kernels + Talisman
  - Artifact: 250 Kernels + Ring
  - Relic: 500 Kernels + Artifact
  - Heirloom: 1,000 Kernels + Relic
- Feast I Books
  - Applied to Farming Tools
  - Grants +0.5-2.5 Overbloom
  - Costs 25 Kernels
- Finn's Focaccia
  - Consumable
  - Grants +5 Overbloom for the rest of the Grand Feast
  - Costs 50 Kernels
- Ted's Contact Baked In Bread
  - Unlocks Ted's Abiphone Contact
  - One of the three in-season crops will give 10% more Seasonings, he'll call you to let you know which one
  - Costs 500 Kernels
- Scott's Contact Baked In Bread
  - Unlocks Scott's Abiphone Contact
  - Allows you to open the Grand Bakery outside of the Grand Feast
  - Costs 500 Kernels
- Bread Bowl yummy
  - Costs 10,000 Kernels
  - Is a super cool thing to give to somebody
### ➥ Removal of Pelt-pocalypse
With the introduction of Finnegan's new Grand Feast perk we're retiring his old "Pelt-pocalypse" perk. Talbot's Shop and the Horse Trapper Animal are now permanently available. In addition to this, we've slightly reworked the Trapper's Crest accessory. It now upgrades an additional time and buffs the amount of Pelts you drop to compensate for the removal of the Mayor perk.
## ► Fossil Essence Shop
Fossil Dust has been turned into Fossil Essence, which can be spent in a brand-new shop! The Fossil Essence Shop is accessed through Researcher Beryl, a new NPC that can be found in the top floor of the Fossil Research Center.
Perks:
- Sleight Of Hand I: Start the Fossil Excavator with an additional charge
- Chilled to the Bone I-X: Increases your chances of fishing up Skeleton Fish in the Crimson Isle by 2-20%
- Dwarven Expertise I-X: Grants +2-20 Dwarven Metal Fortune
- Cut Loose I-V: Killing a ❆ Glacial mob while inside a Glacite Mineshaft grants +0.5-2.5 Cold Resistance (up to x10)
- Prehistorian I-X: Increases the chance of a Fossil Dye spawning in the Fossil Excavator by 1-10%
- Resourceful I-V: Grants a 1-5% chance to not consume keys when looting a Frozen Corpse
In total, you will need 66,250 Fossil Essence to max out the shop.
Additionally, you are now able to purchase a Chisel through this menu for 5 Fossil Essence. Suspicious Scrap is now purchased here for 100 Fossil Essence as well.
### ➥ Fossil Muncher
Meet the Fossil Muncher, a new NPC found in the Glacite Tunnels. Every 24 hours he'll ask for a random Fossil. Giving him this Fossil will reward you with 25,000 Glacite Powder and 100 Fossil Essence.
### ➥ Other Mining Changes
The loot table of the Fossil Excavator has been updated
Fossil Excavator Loot Table
|Item|Amount|Weight|
|-|-|-|
|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone|20|450|
|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone|40|300|
|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone|1|150|
|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone|2|75|
|Flawed Peridot Gemstone|20|450|
|Flawed Peridot Gemstone|40|300|
|Fine Peridot Gemstone|1|150|
|Fine Peridot Gemstone|2|75|
|Flawed Citrine Gemstone|20|450|
|Flawed Citrine Gemstone|40|300|
|Fine Citrine Gemstone|1|150|
|Fine Citrine Gemstone|2|75|
|Flawed Onyx Gemstone|20|450|
|Flawed Onyx Gemstone|40|300|
|Fine Onyx Gemstone|1|150|
|Fine Onyx Gemstone|2|75|
|Fossil Essence|10|400|
|Fossil Essence|20|200|
|Fossil Essence|40|100|

|Enchanted Umber|4|125|
|Enchanted Umber|8|50|
|Enchanted Tungsten|2|250|
|Enchanted Tungsten|4|125|
|Enchanted Tungsten|8|50|

|Enchanted Glacite|4|125|

|Glacite Powder|4000-6000|40|
|Glacite Powder|7000-10,000|20|

|Fossil the Fish|1|15|
|}
- Added +100 Fossil Essence to Dr Stone's rewards for donating a Fossil
- You can now salvage the following items:
  - Suspicious Scrap: 5 Fossil Essence
  - Caged Wisp: 500 Fossil Essence
  - Frosted Scute: 500 Fossil Essence
  - Shattered Locket: 1,000 Fossil Essence
  - Prehistoric Egg: 25 Fossil Essence
  - Glacite Chunks: 25 Fossil Essence
  - Fossil the Fish: 25 Fossil Essence
- Buffed the amount of Fossil Essence earned from the Fossil Block from 1 → 4-8
- Citrine Gemstones now prioritize higher value treasures in the Fossil Excavator when highlighting slots
  - Fossil Dye (Highest Priority)
  - Fossil the Fish
  - Paleontologist I Enchantment
  - Glacite Powder / Essence
  - Gemstones/Glacite/Umber/Tungsten (Lowest Priority)
- Halved the Cold Resistance given by the Frozen Skin perk in the Ice Essence Shop
- Added all Fossils to the Bazaar
- Improved the zone entry message for the Glacite Tunnels and the Fossil Research Center
## ► Farming Toolkit
One of the most popular suggestions we've gotten recently is for a farming equivalent to the Hunting Toolkit. It's finally here! You can purchase one for 500 Copper from SkyMart and it'll store your Farming Tools for all crops. Consuming the item now grants access to the /farmingtoolkit menu, where you can store your Farming Tools. You can also access this menu by shift right-clicking the Farming Tool you are currently holding, just like the Hunting Toolkit.
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Carnival Perks
There are now 4 new perks available in Doug's Event Shop at the Carnival to help out with your progression through the Harvest Feast: Natural Talent I-V: Grants a +1-5% chance to find Seasonings during a Harvest Feast Fortunate Feasting I-V: Grants +5-25 Farming Fortune during a Harvest Feast Feast Crashers I-III: Grants +2-6 Bonus Pest Chance during a Harvest Feast Leftovers I-II: Earn +1/+2 Feast Flasks if you reach the final Donation Tier
### ➥ Farming Tool Type
We've recategorized all Hoes and Axes used for farming into a new "Farming Tool" item type. They now share the same reforges and enchantments for simplicity sake. As a direct result of this, we've removed the Sunder enchantment from the game as it was redundant with the functionality of the Harvesting enchantment, but for Axes.
Items Now Classified As "Farming Tools"
- Wooden/Golden/Stone/Iron/Diamond Hoe
- Rookie Hoe
- Basic/Advanced Gardening Hoe
- Basic/Advanced Gardening Axe
- Euclid’s Wheat Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Gauss Carrot Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Pythagorean Potato Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Newton Nether Warts Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Turing Sugar Cane Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Eclipse Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Wild Rose Hoe T1/T2/T3
- Pumpkin Dicer 1.0/2.0/3.0
- Melon Dicer 1.0/2.0/3.0
- Cocoa Chopper
- Cactus Knife
- Fungi Cutter
- Binghoe
We've replaced the Sunder VI drop from Locusts with a new drop: Locust Larva.
### ➥ New Visitor: Taylor
Taylor can now show up on your Garden as a MYTHIC Visitor starting at Garden Level XIV. She'll offer the player one of two cosmetics: the Fast Food Barn Skin or the Jelly Greenhouse Skin.
If she's offering the Barn Skin, she'll ask for lots of Deepfries, Salted Sunflower Seeds, and Enchanted Baked Potatoes. If she's offering the Greenhouse Skin, she'll ask for lots of Floral Gelatin, Melon Juice, and Compacted Wild Roses.
### ➥ Farming Reforge Stone Changes
- Blessed Fruit
  - Moved stats from current Reforge Bonus to base stats (aesthetic change)
  - New Reforge Bonus now grants a chance for you to drop Enchanted Crops when farming
  - Fixed issue with Farming Fortune stat being lower than intended for RARE items with this reforge
- Golden Ball
  - Moved Farming Fortune from Reforge Bonus to base stats (aesthetic change)
- Large Walnut
  - Is now applied to "Farming Tools"
  - Buffed Farming Fortune this reforge provides significantly
  - Now has a Reforge Bonus that grants +5% Sowdust while farming
  - Removed Speed base stats
### ➥ Floating Crystal Changes
- Floating Crystals no longer have an effective radius, they apply their affect to your entire Private Island
- Adjusted the rarities for certain Floating Crystals
- All Floating Crystals now have a new and unique texture
### ➥ Miscellaneous Changes
- Nerfed Pest Farming Fortune scaling by 50% (i.e. Pests will drop 50% less crops from your Farming Fortune, but their base drop amounts are unaffected)
- Designer's Note: Pest Farming has been overwhelming good for the past few months now. Given that we are adding an exciting event with this update with even more drops and even more stats, we think this is a warranted nerf.
- Significantly increased the amount of Sack of Sacks slots given via the Clownfish Collection
  - Small: 3→8 Slots
  - Medium: 6→12 Slots
  - Large: 9→16 Slots
  - Greater: 12→20 Slots
  - Giant: 15→24 Slots
  - Massive: 18→28 Slots
- Rather than setting your Speed Cap to 500, Racing Helmets now grant +100 Speed Cap
- Renamed the "Bonus Attack Speed" stat to just "Attack Speed"
- Fixed having more than 500 Speed not actually making you any faster
## ► Update FAQ
What's coming next month?
Early next month we want to release a fairly large QOL patch and an early game quest to teach players about account security. Later in the month we plan on releasing the Lotus Atoll, a brand new fishing island. You can read the sneak peek for that island HERE.
Where are the Minister perks from the Aura event?
The team is still actively working on implementing all Minister perks from Mayor Aura's event. One or two perks have already been released, as you might have seen with April's Secret Bingo event, and most of the remaining perks will be released bundled together in one update.
When can we expect the second Foraging Island?
We are very deep into development of the second island of Galatea - Torrhus Canyon - and are hoping to release it later this year. You can expect a Sneak Peek for it over the next month or two.
When will we be getting the "new type of combat content"?
The "new type of combat content" is still at early stages of development so we won't share much now, but it is intended to take full advantage of the latest versions of Minecraft and be accessible to players at all stages of the game. We'll share a piece of concept art from the project for now.
What happened to the Guardian Slayer update?
The Guardian Slayer update always required Lotus Atoll to release before it, but now that we have access to latest-version tech we would like to take some time to revisit the Slayer, which was originally built using only 1.8 mechanics. We still plan on releasing it later this year.
What happened to the Great Glacite Lake update?
With the Lotus Atoll and Desecrated Shrine (Guardian Slayer) updates currently planned for fishing, we thought that yet another fishing update so soon would've grown tiresome. Because of this we ultimately split the Great Glacite Lake update into a number of parts, many of which you've already seen released (Frozen Corpse RNG Meter & Milestones, Littlefoot's Den, Fossil Essence Shop). We still plan to release the Great Glacite Lake part of the update, which contains the next TWO pieces of BiS Mining Equipment, Glacite Sea Creatures, new Mineshafts, and an Offering system for the lake that makes it easier to target certain mineshafts, in the future. We apologize for the delay.
That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:
If you notice any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!

# Changelog/2026/April 8
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
[April 8th] Skyblock Gem Price Increase
Updated: April 8, 2026
We've got a pricing update to share today, and we want to walk through it honestly rather than bury it in a changelog.
On April 15th, the amount of gems available in each bundle, as well as the cost of said bundles, will change. Overall, the result means an increase in cost, on a per gem basis, by about 15% across every tier.
Hosting costs, DDoS protection, processing fees have all been climbing steadily, and we've been absorbing those increases year over year.
We've reached the point where the current pricing just doesn't cover what it costs to keep things running and our team paid fairly.
Historically speaking, we've only changed our prices once for ranks in 2014, and it was a price reduction. All of our prices have actually never gone up.
|New Amount|New Price|New Bonus|Old Amount|Old Price|Old Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|700|$5.99|~~|675|$4.99|~~|
|1800|$14.99|1750 + 50|1390|$9.99|1350 + 40|
|3750|$29.99|3500 + 250|3600|$24.99|3375 + 225|
|7600|$59.99|7000 + 600|7300|$49.99|6750 + 550|
|17000|$119.99|14000 + 3000|16400|$99.99|13500 + 2900|
# What is NOT changing:
The gem cost of every item in-game stays exactly the same. A booster cookie still costs 325 gems. Nothing in the store is being repriced. Hypixel Gold is unchanged.
The in-game store will reflect the same updated bundle pricing.
Any gems you already own keep their full value. If you have 1,000 gems right now, you'll have 1,000 gems after April 15. Nothing is being taken away.
This change is about keeping the team together and allows us to keep developing new content, maintaining the network at scale, and making sure the people who build Hypixel every day can keep doing it sustainably.
That's the tradeoff, and we think it's the right call.

# Changelog/2026/August 11/1
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 11] SkyBlock Patch Notes 2
Updated: August 11, 2026
Hello!
We have more fixes ready for you today, as follows:
### ➥ Shard Fusion Fixes
- Fixed Shard Fusion not prioritizing shards with a higher rarity.
  - The Shard Fusion system before Torrhus prioritized ID Fusion before Special Fusions.
  - This is now no longer the case, Special Fusions will appear before ID Fusions.
Quick Note: It is intended that you can no longer ID-Fusion into the Power Dragon shard as it defeats the purpose of the intended obtainment method (M7). This reasoning is also why we made similar changes to some other shards on Torrhus Canyon's release, being able to ID-Fuse into certain shards completely bypasses the intended obtainment method.
Regarding the Power Dragon Shard:
- You can still obtain the Power Dragon shard from Hellwisp through the usage of a Chameleon shard, that is intended behavior.
- If the current obtainment method of the Power Dragon shard (M7) is inadequate, please explain why that's the case, and suggest potential solutions or improvements that don't outright bypass the intended obtainment method.
### ➥ Lava Bouncing in F7/M7
PSA: Following these changes, you should play on Minecraft Version 26.1+ for optimal lava jump behavior.
- Adjusted the Lava Bouncing mechanic in F7/M7 to be closer to existing behavior:
  - There are now two types of lava jumps, regular and super.
  - The regular jump height is slightly higher than before (in 1.21.11 and lower).
  - Looking upwards (pitch of -40 or "lower") triggers a super lava bounce, letting you bounce 30% higher than a regular jump.
Let us know if some/all of these parameters need further adjusting.
- We can lower the default jump height if that's preferred.
  - After doing some testing in Goldor's phase, we think there's a chance the new slightly higher jump height may be preferred -- the old jump height was insufficient at times to bounce back to certain platforms.
- We can adjust the pitch required to trigger a super lava jump.
- We can adjust the height of the super lava jump, if necessary.
EDIT: Jump heights have been adjusted to be higher compared to the initial roll.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2026/August 19

[August 19] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 19, 2026
19th August Update: We are aware of some issues the Bestiary changes have had with certain Critter Bestiaries (Hivethief, Pollendart, Lotus Atoll Critters), causing some to require more kills than intended or to track incorrectly. We will have a fix out for this soon!
Hey all! We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock, containing further improvements to the 0.27 Release!
- Added a "Uniqueness Score" to the Critter Safari that gives all players +5,000 Hunting XP for each Critter caught
  - Designer Note: This change means you can get up to 185,000 Hunting XP from Uniqueness Score alone (before factors such as Floor Drops and Hunting Wisdom are factored in). The goal here is for the Critter Safari to become the optimal way for grinding Hunting XP.
- Increased the amount of Hunting XP obtained from Floor Drops:
  - Old: 100-1,000x Hunting XP
  - New: 2,500-10,000 Hunting XP
- Increased the amount of Hunting XP obtained from Charming a mob by 10x
- Added chat feedback when trying to interact with Troodon walls without an Icebreaker
- Added a Hunting XP breakdown to the Safari Reward Summary
- Adjusted the Ticket Exchange menu to now show your current Forest Essence total
- Adjusted the color of Lush Lily Pads to be dark green instead of orange
- Adjusted Hideyho's Bestiary from 75 -> 50 to max
- Adjusted Critter Bestiaries to award bonus Hunting XP instead of Coins
- Reduced Hunting Wisdom granted by Safari Tickets as a result of the implementation of the "Uniqueness Score":
  - Basic: 10 → 0
  - Economy: 25 → 5
  - Premium → 50 → 15
  - First-Class: 100 → 25
- Reduced Hunting XP given by the Fusion Machine by 75%
- Reduced Hunting XP gained from Ghosts and Glacite Walkers by 50%
  - Designer Note: The Fusion Machine and Ghosts were never intended to be optimal ways of levelling your Hunting Skill - the focal point of the fusion machine in particular was always supposed to be fusions, with the Hunting XP just being a little bonus. We allowed these methods to exist initially as Hunting XP gain was slow, but now that the Critter Safari is capable of serving as a healthier alternative we wish to entice players into
- The Safari Manager will now kick players from the instance if they do not enter the Critter Safari within 30s
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are still balancing and patching this release. You can expect some further changes to the Torrhus Canyon side of the update soon.

# Changelog/2026/August 20
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 20] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 20, 2026
Hello!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
- Adjusted multipliers for the majority of crops and mutations in the Greenhouse
  - Designer Note: Certain mutations have been producing tens of millions of coins every day for very little effort, outshining other money-making methods for the amount of time invested. Looking over the last 7 days, mutation income has accounted for 10.17% of all coins generated.
  We think that number is too large for just mutations, as the effort does not reflect the amount of income it produces. A lot of mutations have been changed, including nerfs to Ashwreath, but also major buffs to underutilized mutations aswell like Snoozling for example. Mutations should now be a lot closer when it comes to npc coins generated, which should make most of the mutations viable again. Some variance in value generated is kept, as to account for differences in growth cycles, effort and rarity. We will monitor the impact of these changes and if necessary make further changes in the future. Mutation Changes
|Mutation|Old Multiplier|New Multiplier|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat|0.2|0.18|
|Carrot|0.2|0.125|
|Potato|0.2|0.125|

|Gloomgourd|0.175|0.2|
|Shadevine|0.225|0.26|
|Choconut|0.25|0.25|
|Veilshroom|0.175|0.15|
|Ashwreath|0.3|0.15|
|Witherbloom|0.375|0.23|
|Lonelily|2|1.75|

A lower multiplier is effectively less loot from that specific mutation.
- Nerfed Timestalk Clone Shard from 0.5->5 Growth Speed to 0.1 ->1 Growth Speed
- Nerfed Zombuddy Shard from 0.5->5 to 0.1->1 Chance
- Added decay to base crops (72h)
  - Designer Note: Currently, there are quite a few "set and forget" setups especially for common mutations like Ashwreath, effectively only ever needing to harvest the mutations without ever having to replant your Greenhouse. This is partly because most base crops do not have decay timers, effectively leaving them around indefinitely once they are grown. By adding a decay value we can expect people having to replant their setups every once in a while, instead of relying on a setup in which you just harvest mutations every once in a while.
- Reduced rarity of Turtlellini to Rare
- Adjusted Glasscorn to drop more cactus, but less potatoes
- Base crop drops from Shellfruit are now split into Turtlellini and Shellfruit
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE!

[August 24] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 24, 2026
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock, containing further improvements to the 0.27 Release, as well as some more general fixes.
- Added the "Marshroom" reforge stone to Vinesap VIII, which can apply the "Marshy" reforge to Equipment
- Added the "Timber" stat and unified Petalfall, Timber HOTF Perk, and Woodpecker Shard systems under it
- Added the Timid Mob Type, which is applied to mobs that typically flee/hide from the player:
  - Blue Jay
  - Blue Jerry
  - Chuckwalla
  - Coralot
  - Diamond Goblin
  - Duplico
  - Goldolot
  - Golden Goblin
  - Golden Jerry
  - Green Jerry
  - Grinch
  - Hideonfloor
  - Hideonwall
  - Joydive
  - Nozzlenose
  - Pangolin
  - Purple Jerry
  - Rockmite
  - Scared Skeleton
  - Scrappy
  - Sepialot
  - Shyworm
  - Trapper Animals
- Added all Pots of Honeycomb to the Bazaar
- Added the Forest's Favor as a 0.02% drop chance from Helix Tree Gifts. Upgrades Karma V -> VI
- Added Torrhus Sea Creature Shards to the Huntrap Pool:
  - Spring Shallows:
  - Solar Shard: 75 Weight
  - Sepialot Shard: 100 Weight
  - Goldolot Shard: 25 Weight
  - Water Snake Shard: 50 Weight
  - Haggard Shard (NEW): 100 Weight
  - Brineling Shard (NEW): 50 Weight
  - Sprawl Shard (NEW): 25 Weight
- Spring Depths:
  - Ember Shard: 50 Weight
  - Sepialot Shard: 100 Weight
  - Stingray Shard: 50 Weight
  - Goldolot Snake Shard: 25 Weight
  - Water Snake Shard: 50 Weight
  - Torrid Shard (NEW): 100 Weight
  - Silkbreeze Shard (NEW): 25 Weight
  - Giant Isopod Shard (NEW): 10 Weight
- Added Sea Brine as a drop to the Brineling:
  - Used to craft the Brine Tonic
    - Effect: Grants +2.5 Charm Chance on Moonglade Sea Creatures and Torrhus Sea Creatures.
- Improved the itemlore of the Reinforced Netting and Sublime Silk
- Improved the Sweep formula - Swoop's menu now reflects the new, more intuitive formula
- Increased the scaling of "Two-for-one" HOTF perk from 0.1-10% -> 0.25% -> 25%
- Increased the amount of Foraging XP given as part of Miria's Contest Rewards:
  - COMMON: 20,000 -> 15,000
  - UNCOMMON: 30,000 -> 30,000 (Unchanged)
  - RARE: 40,000 -> 45,000
  - EPIC: 50,000 -> 60,000
  - LEGENDARY: 60,000 -> 75,000
  - MYTHIC: 60,000 -> 75,000
  - SPECIAL: 60,000 -> 75,000
  - DIVINE: 60,000 -> 75,000
  - Adjusted HOTF XP Gain from Tree Gifts:
  - Fig: 10 -> 10 (Unchanged)
  - Mangrove: 15 -> 20
  - Helix: 20 -> 30
- Adjusted Shards from Honeyhives to no longer be affected by Hunting Fortune
- Adjusted HONEYCOMB! messages to now mention the general location of the tree that attracted a Critter
- Adjusted all Pots of Honeycomb to now only have 1 charge to prevent Auction House scams:
- Increased crafting output of Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb from 1x -> 8x
- Increased Enchanted Helix Logs needed to craft Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb from 16x -> 32x
- Increased crafting output of Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb from 1x -> 8x
- Increased amount of Behemoth Pots of Honeycomb dropped by the Beeheemoth from 1x -> 8x
- Adjusted Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb recipe to output 1x -> 8x of the item
- Adjusted Stealth enchantment:
  - Designer Note: Now scales from I-V and caps out at 60% instead of 100% with its Endcap as we didn't feel that entirely bypassing the behavior of Timid mobs with 100% reliability was healthy. In exchange for this numerical nerf, it now applies to all mobs with the Timid mob type (Grinches, Golden/Diamond Goblins, etc).
- Adjusted Pangolins to now only spawn within Pangolin Hideaways
- Adjusted Lassos to now use paper as their base item instead of leads
- Adjusted some sources of point gain for Miria's Contests:
  - Increased COMMON Shards from 3 -> 9
  - Increased UNCOMMON Shards from 6 -> 25
  - Increased RARE Shards from 12 -> 50
  - Increased EPIC Shards from 24 -> 100
  - Increased LEGENDARY Shards from 48 -> 200
  - Increased Per Catch from 4 -> 10
  - Increased Per Good Catch from 25 -> 50
  - Increased Per Great Catch from 75 -> 250
  - Increased Per Outstanding Catch from 250 -> 750
  - Increased Honeycomb from N/A -> 1
  - Reduced Per Mob Killed from 8 -> 4
  - Reduced per Ruby Veilshroom from 12 -> 8
  - Removed point gain from Sea Lumies
- Adjusted Distant Echo to increase the level of Stealth enchantment instead of Karma
- Adjusted Lassos to use the modern item.lead.break sound when breaking
- Adjusted Fusion Recipe for Queen Ant Shard from Ant + Elusive Family -> Ant + Queen Bee
- Adjusted Fusion Recipe for Special Zealot Shard from Zealot + Elusive Family -> Obtainable via Generic Fusion
- Adjusted all Torrhus Sea Creatures to be Charm only
  - Designer Note: Charm-only shards are an important part of the Hunting ecosystem as they are the cornerstone through which the Charm Chance stat, Salts, and Elusive shard systems operate. We specifically chose to make Sea Creatures charm-only two reasons: to avoid disrupting the fishing gameplay loop AND because you need to kill them more times than any other mobs on the island (they have more drops that you need and their higher bestiaries are reflective of this)
- Renamed Woodpecker's Attribute from Forest Felling → Helix Timber - now only affects Helix Trees
- Removed Tree the Fish from Helix Tree Gifts and Torrhus Canyon /pity
- Removed Mana costs from Fishing Nets
- Fixed an issue causing Grizzly Bears to despawn earlier than intended
- Fixed an issue causing the Fusion Machine to prioritize lower-tier Shards
- Reduced Shellfruit drops to only be Cocoa Beans
- Removed the "Elusive" Shard Family - all mobs with the Elusive Mob Type that CAN drop a Shard will ALWAYS drop it on kill
  - Sanger's dialogue has been updated to better explain this mechanic
- Fixed the Attribute Menu and Hunting Box repeatedly setting you back to Page 1 and resetting filters
- Fixed the Queen Ant Shard not working against Queen Ants
- Fixed Beeheemoths not granting points towards Miria's Contests
- Fixed Bunbun Bestiary using the incorrect icon
- Fixed an issue causing players to be unable to Lasso mobs if standing too close
- Fixed several Critter Bestiaries awarding Magic Find instead of Hunting Fortune
- Fixed several Lotus Atoll Critters using the incorrect Bestiary Brackets
- Fixed Melon armor mentioned still in descriptions.
- Fixed Hocus Pocus cipher not being rift-transferrable.
- Fixed reclaim only returning 1 Farming For Dummies.
- Fixed Feast Milestones menu showing 0 progress for locked milestones.
- Fixed Hoppity egg in Dwarven Mines saying "beside Aristocrat Passage".
- Fixed Hoppity call shop not taking from bank.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2026/August 4
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
0.27
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.27 - Torrhus Canyon & Critter Safari
Updated: August 4, 2026
Hello! Today, for the first time since Hypixel SkyBlock's release, we are adding two new islands to the game in one of the largest updates SkyBlock has ever seen. Sound exciting? Then read on for all the details!
## ► Torrhus Canyon
Torrhus Canyon is the second Foraging Island comprising the region known as Galatea. Accessible from a launch pad in the Moonglade Marsh upon reaching Heart of the Forest 4, there is much to do and see here!
### ➥ Torrhus Chapters
Upon entering the island for the first time, look for Helia just down Torrhus Path. She will introduce you to the Torrhus Chapters, which you can use to inform your exploration of the island.
After meeting Helia, you can progress down the Spring Path towards Miria - the second Starlyn sister and host of this island's Starlyn Contests! She'll explain to you how her contests work, as well as how she's entered a business partnership with the Critter Safari, before telling you to go chop some wood.
### ➥ 3 New Collections
Speaking of wood, Torrhus Canyon is home to three new collections - Helix Log, Ruby Veilshroom, and Honeycomb. Each of these collections has some upgrades to offer to the gear you first obtained on the Moonglade Marsh, so be sure to check them all out!
Helix Logs are obtained from cutting the many Helix Trees that are native to Torrhus Canyon. These trees are the toughest you've come across to this point, so be sure to have a lot of Sweep! Honeycomb, on the other hand, is primarily gathered from the various Honeyhives found around the island. Be careful, though - you never know what you might find when you stick your hand into a Honeyhive! Last but not least, Ruby Veilshrooms can be obtained from killing Torrhus Sea Creatures up in Torrhus Springs.
### ➥ Torrhus Springs
Torrhus Springs, one of the tallest points of the entire island, is an open spring basin that is frequented by seasoned divers. If you can withstand the pressure, dive on down and you'll find yourself surrounded by fascinating Critters of all shapes, sizes, and temperaments. Cast your rod in, and you might just catch some Torrhus Sea Creatures.
Keep an eye out for the various offshoot caves found throughout the depths - divers have reported finding one cave housing an entire ant colony, another that is home to a strange type of fishing hotspot, and more... If you can make it all the way to the bottom, perhaps you'll even find something no other diver could?
### ➥ Heart of the Forest Tier 8
Heart of the Forest now goes up to 8 tiers! An overall revamp to the tree has been done with the goal of moving some important perks further down the tree for new players to use sooner (those changes are listed further down the thread), and whispers have been split into two types:
- Forest Whispers -> Gained from cutting Fig Trees and Mangrove Trees. Used to upgrade HOTF 1-3 perks.
- Desert Whispers-> Gained from cutting Helix Trees. Used to upgrade HOTF 4-8 perks.
  - Designer Note: Players with in excess of 12,500 Forest Whispers phave had them converted to Desert Whispers at a 1:1 rate.
### ➥ Charm Chance
Charm Chance is now an official Hunting Stat! As is the case with most "chance" stats, such as Sea Creature Chance and Treasure Chance, the stat scales from 0% -> 100%, making you more likely to drop Attribute Shards from viable mobs as it increases.
## ► Critter Safari
Speaking of hunting, the Critter Safari is now open for business! Grab your ticket from Miria, travel to the Safari Entrance on the right-hand side of Torrhus Canyon, and get on the next blimp to travel to the game's first dedicated Hunting Island!
### ➥ Entry
Players may redeem one of their Safari Tickets - which are obtained from placing highly in Miria's Contests - to enter the Critter Safari alone or with a group of up to three friends.
Once inside, players are free to go together or alone as they explore the Safari's four biomes (Icy, Canyon, Forest, and Haunted), armed only with a set of Critter Capsules that they can use to catch any Critters they come across. When caught, Critters will drop Attribute Shards, which are sent to your Hunting Box for you to claim once you have left the Critter Safari.
### ➥ 150~ New Attributes!
Not only do both Torrhus Canyon and the Critter Safari boast more unique mobs than any other island in the game, but every mob species on those islands also drops a unique attribute shard for you to collect!
In addition to all of the new mobs, there are also plenty of new shards obtainable only through attribute fusion. With so many shards also comes many new shard families - here's a shard from the crime family, for example!
As a result of all of these new Attributes, the total amount of Attributes across the game has approximately doubled. For now, we have kept Jade Dragon's "Apex Predator" perk untouched, but it may need to be adjusted to be flat Sweep instead of percentage-based when we inevitably add even more Attributes in the future.
### ➥ Safari Milestones
Before entering the Critter Safari for the first time you will be given the Safari Belt. This nifty piece of equipment is the only thing you can take into the Safari and, though it doesn't look like much right now, it has the potential to be great.
As you catch more and more Critters in each biome, you will progress through the Safari Milestones. Each milestone reached will grant bonus stats to your Safari Belt, quickly turning it from a useless item into the best belt in the game for Hunting and Foraging:
- Canyon Milestones: Increases Rarity and grants Hunting Wisdom
- Haunted Milestones: Grants Hunting Fortune
- Icy Milestones: Grants Cold Resistance
- Forest Milestones: Grants Foraging Fortune
You can check your Safari Milestones progress anytime by talking to Andrea by the Safari Entrance in Torrhus Canyon.
### ➥ Sparkling Critters
All Critters have a base 1/4,096 chance to instead spawn as a sparkling variant - something which is indicated by a different color scheme, a "SPARKLING" prefix, and a sparkling trail that follows the critter around.
All Sparkling Critters grant 10x the rewards of a regular Critter and drop a Rainbow Feather, which can be used to increase the current level of any pet by 1 (cannot bypass the cap). Additionally, players who have already completed the base Critterdex will gain access to Tobias' Sparkling Critterdex, where they can take on the optional challenge (no SBXP attached) of trying to catch all 37 sparkling critters in exchange for some Cosmetic rewards along the way.
Will you be the first to catch all 37 Sparkling Critter variants?
## ► Other Changes
### ➥ Foraging
- Added Bestiaries for all Moonglade Marsh mobs, being retroactive wherever possible
- Adjusted "Found in the Grass" drops (e.g Mudworm Shard) into a game-wide "Floor Drops" system
- Added +50 Helix Fortune as a possible reward from the "Lottery" HOTF perk
- Removed Fish the Fish task from Chapter 7 of Torrhus Chapters
- Adjusted Swoop's name color and added a "SWOOP MILESTONES" header above him
- Reduced Fig Hew rarity from RARE -> UNCOMMON
- Reduced Figstone Splitter rarity from EPIC -> RARE
- Reduced Enchanted Fig Log rarity from RARE -> UNCOMMON
- Reduced Enchanted Mangrove Log rarity from RARE -> UNCOMMON
- Reduced Figstone rarity from EPIC -> RARE
- Reduced Mangcore rarity from EPIC -> RARE
- Adjusted "Half Empty" and "Half Full" HOTF perks to function island-wide instead of in a 16-block proximity
- Adjusted Travel Scroll to Murkwater Loch to mention island as Moonglade Marsh instead of Galatea
- Renamed Starlyn Prize -> Agatha's Prize
- Added text to Shiniest indicating it's purchased from Agatha's Shop
- Renamed the "Galatea" section of the Bestiary to "Moonglade Marsh"
- Added the "Critter" Mob Type, which is given to all mobs that cannot be killed, only hunted (e.g Mossybit)
- Added Mob Types to several Moonglade Marsh Critters that were missing them:
  - Birries: Critter
  - Spike: Critter, Aquatic, Animal
  - Shellwise: Critter, Aquatic, Animal
  - Joydive: Critter, Aquatic, Animal
  - Hideonleaf: Critter, Woodland
  - Invisibug: Critter, Airborne
  - Mossybit: Critter, Woodland, Animal
  - Dreadwing: Critter, Woodland, Airborne
  - Bambuleaf: Critter, Woodland, Animal

  - Lumisquid: Critter, Aquatic, Animal
- Adjusted NPC sell prices of Galatea wood:
  - Fig: 7 -> 7 (Unchanged)
  - Mangrove: 8 -> 15
- Removed most sources of % Sweep and replaced them with other perks:
  - COTF Level 2: +5% Sweep -> +1 Axe Ability Level
  - COTF Level 4: +10% Sweep -> +5 Tracking on Tree Gifts
  - "Lottery" HOTF Perk: +5% Sweep -> 10 Sweep
  - Agatha's Power I-V: +1-5% Sweep -> +5-25 Sweep
- Removed Flexbones from Treasure tables. They now have a 25% chance to replace a Treasure Catch while in the Tomb Floodway:
  - GOOD -> 1x Flexbone
  - GREAT -> 2-4x Flexbone
  - OUTSTANDING -> 8x Flexbone
- Fixed the Throwing Axe Penalty being applied incorrectly, causing it to break less logs than intended
- Hid HP from all mobs with the "Critter" mob type as they cannot be damaged
- Reduced Tree Gift Milestones for all Tree types:
  - I: 10 -> 10
  - II: 50 -> 25
  - III: 250 -> 100
  - IV: 500 -> 250
  - V: 1,000 -> 500
  - VI: 2,500 -> 1,000
  - VII: 5,000 -> 2,500
- Adjusted Jade Dragon Egg cost:
  - Figstone: 100 -> 64
  - Mangcore: 100 -> 64
  - Helixis: 0 -> 64
- Added Stretching Sticks to the Bazaar
- Adjusted Sea Lumies to now give Foraging XP
- Reorganized the HOTF tree to make certain perks more accessible for newer players. From left to right, the order is:
  - HOTF 1: Sweep
  - HOTF 2: Damage Boost, Luck of the Forest, Foraging Fortune, Collector, Axe Toss
  - HOTF 3: Deep Waters, Hunter's Luck, Galatea's Might
  - HOTF 4: Lottery, Foraging Madness, Iron Lungs, 250 Gifts, Daily Wishes, Early Bird, Precision Cutting
  - HOTF 5: Tree Whisperer, Center of the Forest, Free Trial
  - HOTF 6: Homing Axe, Forest Fisher, Str. Boost, Starlyn Supreme, Spd. Boost, Efficient Forager, Maniac Slicer
  - HOTF 7: Half Empty, Ricochet, Half Full
  - HOTF 8: Monster Hunter, Forest Speed, Essence Fortune, Timber, Two-for-one, Forest Strength, Beekeeper
    - Designer Note: We will keep an eye on the impacts of this reshuffle and will make further adjustments if necessary, such as moving both the Speed and Strength Boosts further down the tree.
- Increased Fig Tree toughness from 7 -> 10
- Adjusted Swoop's dialogue to correctly mention Fig Trees now have a Toughness of 10
- Added +10 Sweep as a base stat to the Frog Pet
- Improved itemlore of HOTF Token message in /HOTF to explain how Desert Whispers are obtained
- Unsoulbound some items:
  - Frog Treats
  - Sloth Claws
### ➥ Hunting
- Increased Hunting Skill Cap from 25 -> 50
- Renamed Hunter Fortune -> Hunting Fortune
- Adjusted Backwater Bayou's Huntrap table:
  - Dumpster Diver Shard (NEW!): 2000 Weight
  - Bayou Sludge Shard (NEW!): 1000 Weight

  - Field Mouse -> Field Mouse Shard
    - Pest Shard, renamed
    - Gains "Trash Lover" family, no longer "Bug" family
    - Standardized behavior to "Grants +0.5-5 Overbloom on Pests"
  - Lunar Moth -> Lunar Moth Shard
    - Some of these Shards were moved from prexisting sources, others have had their behavior standardized.
- Moved Lapis Creeper Shard from Combat -> Enchanting category
- Moved Emerald Dye source from mining Emeralds -> hunting Critters in the Critter Safari
- Adjusted Dodo Shard:
  - Old: Grants +1-10 Pet Luck
  - New: Grants +0.5-5 Tracking
  - Moved category from Taming -> Global
- Reduced Tracking granted by Private Island Beacon from +1-5 -> 0.5-2.5
- Added some pre-existing Shards to the new "Trash Lover" family:
  - Crow
  - Seagull
- Added some pre-existing Shards to the new "Poltergeist" family:
  - Mimic
- Added an ability to the Entangler Lasso:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Forest Shards caught with this lasso.
- Added an ability to the Medium Fishing Net:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Water Shards caught with this net.
- Added an ability to the Medium Pocket Black Hole:
  - Grants +2.5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this black hole!
- Adjusted ability on the Large Pocket Black Hole:
  - Grants +5 Hunting Fortune on Combat Shards caught with this black hole!
- Adjusted the Stamina Bars of Critters to now scale with Shard rarity:
  - COMMON Shard -> 1 Stamina Bar
  - UNCOMMON Shard -> 2 Stamina Bars
  - RARE Shard -> 3 Stamina Bars
  - EPIC Shard -> 4 Stamina Bars
  - LEGENDARY Shard -> 5 Stamina Bars
    - Also adjusted colors of Stamina Bars to follow the item rarity system
- Added a Hunting Wisdom Booster that can be purchased from Rosemary for 250 Forest Essence
- Players who spawn mobs from Tree Gifts will now always get Loot Share on them
- Improved itemlore of Pocket Black Holes
### ➥ Other
- Moved Fish the Fish from Moonglade Marsh -> 1/2000 Treasure drop from Torrhus Canyon
- Adjusted Crow Pet's "Quick Hands" perk:
  - Old: Lowers the cooldown of your abilties by 0.15-15%.
  - New: Lowers the cooldown of your Weapon abilities by 0.15-15%.
- Adjusted Dark Cacao Truffle from +1 Global Fortune -> +1 Cocoa Fortune
  - Designer Note: Items giving Global Fortune is not compatible with our vision for the game in the long run so we decided to make this change now, in the same update where we're adding various other chocolate sinks to the Chocolate Factory.
- Added a skin to Treasure Hoarders
- Allowed Auto Recombobulator to work on Summoning Eyes
- Reworked Bal's "Chimney" perk and renamed it to "Cannibal":
  - Old: Reduce Pickaxe Ability Cooldowns by 1-10%.
  - New: Increases damage dealt to Bal by +1-100%.
    - Designer Note: Zombie Miner now drops a Shard that reduces your Pickaxe Cooldown.
- Removed Combat XP from Trevor the Trapper mobs
- Removed the 1.5x Pet XP boost from Mining
- Adjusted itemlore on all Vacuums to specify it only stores Pests caught in The Garden in the vacuum bag
- Renamed Worm -> Stoneworm and gave them a new texture
- Adjusted stat icon of Pressure Resistance from Glass Bottle -> Water Bottle
- Removed the Slayer and Bartender discount perks from players who had them from the Community Project
  - Players who previously had the perks have been given enough Shards to max out those respective Attributes. Claim them from the Community Projects menu!
    - Designer Note: It felt like it was time to make these perks available to everyone, as many years have gone by since that Community Project concluded. We plan to take the same Attribute conversion path with other Community Project-exclusive perks in the future.
- Increased the damage dealt by Vacuums:
  - SkyMart Vacuum: 100 → 100
  - SkyMart Turbo Vacuum: 120 → 150
  - SkyMart Hyper Vacuum: 150 → 200
  - Infinivacuum: 200 → 300
  - Infinivacuum Hooverius: 200 → 400
- Increased the damage bonus granted by Bookworm's Favorite Book from +10 -> 20
- Hid "Talk to Susan" scoreboard objective from all players not in The Hub
- Added some new Secret Achievements
- Adjusted "Dragon Piper" Draconic Essence Shop perk:
  - Old: Gain a +10% chance to spawn a Superior Dragon if you placed a Summoning Eye (Unstackable).
  - New: Summoning Eyes you drop have a +5% chance to be recombobulated.
- Players placing Recombobulated Summoning Eyes now increases the chance of getting a Superior Dragon:
  - 0/8: 4% -> 4% (Unchanged)
  - 1/8: 4% → 12.5%
  - 2/8: 4% → 25.0%
  - 3/8: 4% → 37.5%
  - 4/8: 4% → 50.0%
  - 5/8: 4% → 62.5%
  - 6/8: 4% → 75.0%
  - 7/8: 100% → 87.5%
  - 8/8: 100% (Unchanged)
- Designer Note: A small amount of Attributes that were present on Alpha, as well as some changes to Farming, are not included in this release.
## ► SkyBlock OST
While exploring Torrhus Canyon you'll get to hear the gorgeous soundtrack for the island composed by Oscar Garvin (you may know him as the composer for Hytale). He has also composed other songs that have been added for the Hub, the Garden, and the Moonglade Marsh. These are not note block songs, the recently added SkyBlock Resource Pack has allowed us to add fully orchestral songs to SkyBlock!
Make sure you have SkyBlock Music enabled in your SkyBlock Settings and to turn your Jukebox/Note Blocks volume up so that you can hear the OST!
## ► Bestiary Changes
Following the conclusion of the recent SkyBlock Designer Q&A, we have had a lot of discussions about the place of the Bestiary within SkyBlock. Since its initial release as part of SkyBlock 0.11.2, the Bestiary has been through several permutations - starting off as something that was borderline impossible to max but, with the introduction of SkyBlock XP, slowly transitioned into something that was more feasible to complete.
### ➥ Bestiary Issues
However, there were still many problems with the Bestiary that quickly became apparent to anyone trying to max it:
1. The Bestiary is usually the last aspect of a piece of content that you will max out - it inflates how long you need to play a piece of content for.
2. Early-game Bestiaries (e.g. Lapis Zombie) were inherently unhealthy due to forcing veterans to compete with new players for kills.
3. While some Bestiaries had been modernized and weren't actually too difficult, there were many outliers that still demanded far too much player time.
4. By the time you max out a Bestiary, getting all of its rewards, you're completely done with that content, so you never get to reap the bonus Magic Find, Coins, XP Orbs, and other rewards on that mob.
### ➥ Resulting Bestiary Reductions
As a result of the issues identified above, we have made the decision to slash Bestiaries across the game by a massive amount. Some examples of the type of changes you can expect to see include:
1. Lapis Zombie decreased from 3,000 -> 200
2. Lonely Spider decreased from 25,000 -> 4,000
3. Butterfly reduced from 1,000 -> 100
4. Dragons reduced from 1,000 -> 250
5. COMMON Sea Creatures decreased from various -> 1,000
6. MYTHIC Sea Creatures decreased from 100 -> 50
If you wish to see the entire changelog for the Bestiary changes, which affects the majority of mobs in the game, CLICK HERE.
### ➥ Changing our Bestiary Philosophy
Going forward, bestiaries will be designed to be more lenient - we will look for players to max their bestiary at a reasonable point when playing content so that they can then use the bestiary bonuses - such as the magic find and coin bonus - to improve their rates when grinding for a mob's materials or RNG drop.
What we mean is that, just because all Mythic Sea Creature bestiaries now cap at 50 kills, it doesn't mean future Mythic Sea Creatures will be designed so that you've obtained all of their materials and RNG drops by the time you reach that 50th kill. The material grind for each creature can still vary significantly - the goal is simply to make bestiaries more consistent, so they're something you'll typically complete early in the overall grind rather than as the very last milestone.
We apologize to anyone who ended up taking the time to grind out an older Bestiary just for it to be slashed - we will be more mindful when designing Bestiaries going forwards and the reduced brackets should make things significantly more reasonable by default.
## ► Texture Adjustments
Following community feedback, we have adjusted the textures for the following items:
- Terminator (pictured above)
- Aspect of the Void
## ► What's Next?
Here's a little sneak peek at what we have planned to release in the near future:
### ➥ The Ministers Update
The Minister Update is currently deep in the QA phase. Once ready, it will be put onto the Alpha Network for an extended period of testing and iteration.
### ➥ Mob Level Improvements
Mob Levels are not very accurate within SkyBlock. For example, both the Zealot Bruiser and the Kalhuiki Tribe Member are currently Level 100, despite the Tribe Member having 15,384× more Health and dealing 320× more damage per hit.
Our improvements to mob levels aim to automatically generate coins, skill XP, XP orbs, and mob levels for every mob in the game that accurately reflect how difficult the mob is to kill.
This is done by taking into account contextual factors such as a mob's health, damage, and place within the game's progression (for example, whether it is a Boss or Miniboss, whether it is a Sea Creature and, if so, which tier it belongs to, and so on), though manual rewards can still be set.
The goal here is to ensure players are appropriately rewarded for defeating stronger enemies, which should make areas of the game such as early/mid-game fishing & combat better for Skill XP, and for the visual "mob level" above every mob's head to slowly increase linearly as you progress through the game.
### ➥ QoL Bundle
We are always working on QoL changes to make the game easier for everyone. This upcoming bundle contains key changes such as making bulk-buying from Jax easier, reducing the crafting recipes for Exportable Carrots and the Blood Donor Artifact, and more!
And that's all for today! Grab your most trustworthy axe and we'll see you over on Torrhus Canyon!

# Changelog/2026/August 6
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 6] 0.27 Hotfixes & Healing Revamp Adjustments
Updated: August 6, 2026
Hello! Today we squashed some bugs and made some adjustments relating to Healing Revamp.
## ► 0.27 Bug Fixes​
- Fixed the Bestiary for Torrid, Silkbreeze, and Giant Isopod requiring more kills than intended.
  - Giant Isopod: Bracket: 7 → 8
    - MYTHIC Sea Creature, 50 kills required
  - Silkbreeze: Bracket: 5 → 6
    - LEGENDARY Sea Creature, 250 kills required
  - Torrid: Bracket: 5 → 7, Tiers: 15 → 20
    - EPIC Sea Creature, 500 kills required
## ► Healing Revamp Changes​
### ➥ Mastiff-Related Changes​
- Extended the duration of Radiant Power Orb and Mana Flux Power Orb from 30s -> 1 minute
- Fixed Mastiff Armor's "heal on hit" set bonus not proccing.
- Removed the negative Defense and Crit Damage debuff on Mastiff.
- You now gain +1 Crit Damage for every 2 Defense.
  - This naturally caps at +150 Crit Damage as the set still has a 300 Defense cap.
Designer's Note: The original Mastiff rework intended for the armor to be useful when fighting all tiers of Sven Packmaster. The armor is designed to be tankier than the other alternatives you'd have at that game stage.
We did some further testing and realized a couple things:
1. The full-set healing passive was outright not working.
  1. The heal to vitality ratio on the passive is extremely strong, so missing the effect has big consequences.
2. You need nearly 300 Defense to actually be a decent tank option.
3. You lose quite a bit of damage if you're optimizing for 300 Defense.
  1. (The best way to do so is through armor reforges like "Reinforced", so you lose out on the damage aspect)
  2. At earlier stages of the game, it's quite hard to actually hit 300 Defense!!
All in all, the original design intent of Mastiff being a "tankier option" was not very intuitive to players; the debuffs the armor had only further complicated the matter. We will be attempting to rectify this issue with the above changes. Because Defense (up to 300) is very important for Mastiff Armor, we've added a set bonus that rewards you for building Defense to make this synergy more obvious to players.
### ➥ Other Changes​
- Nerfed the "average damage" of Enderman Slayer as follows:
  - T1: 900 → 800
  - T2: 3,200 → 2,720
  - T3: 9000 → 7,650
  - T4: 13,500 → 11,000
- Fixed Mana Disintegrators not being able to be applied on Power Orbs
- Fixed Discrite not evolving into Spotlite when reaching maximum time (150 hours)
- Drain Changes
  - Now procs once per second.
  - Each proc recovers 0.5/1/1.5/2/2.5 Vitality
    - (this should be a 25% buff from previous)
- Fixed Bone Reaver and Felthorn Reaper having the wrong amount of charges and cooldown.
- Nerfed the damage of Wither Miners
  - Regular: 12,960 -> 6,000
  - Master: 162,000 -> 80,000
- Reverted the change to obtaining Refrigerate.
  - This means Refrigerate I costs 1 Volcanic Rock and 10 North Shards again.
  - (Instead,) you can now obtain up to 5 Volcanic Rocks per wave of Jerry's Workshop event, as each gift pile will feature a Volcanic Rock.
- Having an alive Voidgloom Seraph in the area no longer blocks Enderman spawns in the region.

# Changelog/2026/February 17
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[February 17] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: February 17, 2026
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
- Added the Hub Revamp City Project.
- Delicate Enchantment no longer prevents breaking crops inside the Greenhouse.
- Dying to the Void in the Garden now respawns you at the default spawn location.
- Fixed Garden Chip redemption only showing 1 as quantity even when redeeming multiple at a time.
- Fixed Mutations with Fermento as a requirement not showing their spreading conditions.
- Halved Soggybud water siphon thresholds.
- Changed the way the offline mutation grace period is calculated. It no longer stops mutations from growing even if they had enough water to survive. Instead, freshly grown mutations while offline will now only stop growing if they were about to dry out, effectively increasing how long they can grow while offline.
- Fixed giving the Bachelor's Rose to Jerry. This should work on the Jerry on private islands, in the Hub during the winter event, and on the Winter Island.
- Fixed Emissary Sisko and the Wizard not accepting the Bachelor's Rose.
- Fixed crops in The Garden growing slower than expected after harvesting them.
- Prevented cocooning for mobs that come from Greenhouse mutations.
- Fixed Melon Dicers with Silk Touch not gaining any tool exp.
- Fixed the tops of trees in the Barn plot (but outside of the Barn build) being unbreakable due to build height limit.
Update February 18th:
- Dying in any way in the Garden now respawns you at the default spawn location.
- Fixed TNT being able to break the Barn plot.
- Fixed Greenhouse blocks being left behind when changing to a different plot type sometimes.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are still balancing and patching this release. This list is not exhaustive and is subject to change.

# Changelog/2026/February 24
Aliases: 0.24.2
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
0.24.2
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.24.2 - Hub Revamp, Latest Minecraft, Year of the Witch, and more!
Updated: February 24, 2026
Hello! As of today joining Hypixel SkyBlock will require you to be on at least Minecraft version 1.21.9 or above. This is a major step forward for the game and we're excited about what's coming in the not-too-distant future for Hypixel SkyBlock! This update also includes a revamp to The Hub, a new Year of the Witch event, a restructured Museum, an RNG Meter for Frozen Corpses, the end-of-life update for Stranded, and much more. Keep reading to learn all of the details!
## ► Latest MC + Hub Revamp
### ➥ Minecraft 1.21.9+
Joining Hypixel SkyBlock will now require you to be on Minecraft 1.21.9 or higher. Going forward we plan on maintaining support for the two latest game drops, as outlined in this thread. Being on newer versions of Minecraft will help us push SkyBlock to new heights, and we can't wait to show you what we've got in store.
### ➥ Hub Revamp
With the update to latest versions we've taken the time to entirely revamp the layout of The Hub. The island is now more split up into "lanes" for each skill to better direct players down the path they want to go. There are new nooks, crannies, and teasers for what's to come scattered across the new build as well. We couldn't be more pleased with how it turned out!
You may also notice that a few other islands have received some blocks from newer versions as well!
Spoiler: Image Gallery
## NOTE: The first Year of the Witch will be during SkyBlock Year 476, which is a little over 4 days from the posting of this thread (2/24).
Joining annual celebrations for Pigs and Seals comes the Year of the Witch! Once every 12 SkyBlock Years each mob type will have a rare chance of dropping a special stew ingredient. Gather a stack of an ingredient to brew a special stew which grants Alchemy XP and some Magic Find towards that Mob Type.
You can also talk to Agnes for access to the Year of the Witch shop. There's a new talisman, two new Mixins, a sack to hold all of your stew ingredients, and a Witch Pet!
### ➥ Other Yearly Event Changes
- Added a Seal Pet to the Year of the Seal shop
- Pig Pet is now found in the Year of the Pig shop instead of crafted from the Raw Porkchop collection
- Shiny Shards are now a guaranteed drop from Shiny Pigs
  - They are now the currency used in the Year of the Pig shop
- Revamped the Year of the Pig shop
  - Shiny Orb : 5,000 Coins
  - Shiny Rod: x128 Shiny Shards (no longer craftable)
  - Dante Ring: 1,000,000 Coins, x128 Shiny Shards
  - The Art of War: 5,000,000 Coins, x1 Enchanted Cooked Porkchop, x256 Shiny Shards
  - COMMON Pig Pet: x32 Enchanted Raw Porkchop, x64 Shiny Shards
  - Small Pig's Ear: x16 Enchanted Raw Porkchop, x32 Shiny Shards
  - Medium Pig's Ear: x32 Enchanted Raw Porkchop, x32 Shiny Shards
  - Large Pig's Ear: x64 Enchanted Raw Porkchop, x32 Shiny Shards
  - Giant Pig's Ear: x1 Enchanted Cooked Porkchop, x32 Shiny Shards
- Revamped the Pig Pet with the goal of making it better for doing Shiny Pigs and Potato Farming
- Revamped the Flying Pig Pet Item to give +40 Potato Fortune and +20 Speed
- Adjusted the recipes of the Potato Ring and Blood God Sigil
- Added Shiny Pigs to the Bestiary
- Adjusted Piggy's dialogue
- Reduced rarity of Shiny Shards from LEGENDARY to RARE
- Reduced rarity of Shiny Orbs from LEGENDARY to COMMON
- Renamed Dante Talisman and Dante Ring to Dante's Talisman and Dante's Ring respectively
- Revamped the Shiny Pig loot table (see below)

## ► Frozen Corpse RNG Meter
With this update we've introduced an RNG Meter for particularly unlucky players who are looting Frozen Corpses.
### ➥ RNG Meter
Looting Frozen Corpses will give various amounts of RNG Meter XP:
- Lapis Corpses: 500 XP
- Umber/Tungsten Corpses: 2,500 XP
- Vanguard Corpses: 25,000 XP
Like other RNG Meters in the game, you can select a drop through /rng and you'll be guaranteed to drop it in the next Frozen Corpse you open once you hit the required amount of XP. We're granting 10% retroactive RNG Meter XP to all players with this update.
### ➥ New Loot Table
The loot tables for each type of Frozen Corpse has also been re-written, with the intent being for higher tier corpses to be more profitable than they were before. Please note that they have been further adjusted since the initial Alpha phase.

### ➥ Frozen Corpse Milestones
Grandpa Wolf can be found at -56, 123, 332 in the Glacite Tunnels. If you loot enough Frozen Corpses, he'll reward you for your time in the form of several useful perks and a new talisman line. All Frozen Corpses Milestones are retroactive, meaning you won't have to get frostbite once again if you've already put the work in.
### ➥ Other Mining Changes
- Added the ability to salvage the Frosted Scute for 100 Ice Essence
- Added a 5,000,000 NPC sell price to the Frosted Scute and Shattered Locket
- Added a Worm/Scatha Tablist Widget to the Crystal Hollows.
- Added +2.5 Gemstone Spread to the Pendant of Divan's "Legacy" Tiered Bonus.
- Adjusted Mining Fiestas start times, spreading them more evenly throughout the SkyBlock Year.
  - Fiestas now occur throughout the 2nd, 4th, 6th, 8th, and 10th months of the year.
    - [!] Designer Note: This is not a Minister Perk - those are still to come.
- Adjusted Mort to now ask for a Spirit Bone instead of a Spirit Mask as part of his Abiphone Contact quest.
- Improved clarity of the Gifts from the Departed HOTM Perk's itemlore.
- Adjusted Dead Man's Chest HOTM Perk from +1-50% to +0.5-25%.
- Added a line of "Dwarven Lantern" mining deployables:
  - The Dwarven Lantern can be purchased from Lumina.
  - The Dwarven Lantern can be upgraded to the Mithril Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 2.
  - The Mithril Lantern can be upgraded to the Titanium Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 5.
  - The Titanium Lantern can be upgraded to the Glacite Lantern in The Forge at HOTM 8.
  - The Glacite Lantern can be upgraded to the Will-o-wisp in The Forge at HOTM 10.
    - Crafting the Will-o-wisp requires a Caged Wisp, which is a new drop from Tungsten/Umber and Vanguard Frozen Corpses.
## ► Stranded EOL
Back in August , we started work on an end-of-life update to the Stranded mode, working with the community to gather feedback and address some of its most pressing issues. Today we're presenting you with our final set of proposed changes to Stranded:
- Added the following NPCs to Stranded, they have no requirements unless otherwise stated:
  - Alchemist
  - Einary
    - Einary only appears during the Winter.
  - Terry
  - Malik/Blacksmith
  - Roddy
  - Rusty
* Fishing Merchant will still be added to Stranded in a quick patch after the update releases.
- Removed the Builder's collection requirement.
- Halved the HP and damage of Mobs on Stranded profiles.
- Added Legacy Treecapitator to Stranded.
  - You can convert your existing Treecapitator to Stranded version by placing it in the middle slot of the crafting GUI in the Workbench.
- The Weaponsmith now sells Arrows.
- All Fortunes now work on your Stranded island.
- Fixed Melody's Harp not working on Stranded.
- Enabled access to /mobtypes without visiting Ike while on Stranded.
- The Hex can be accessed on Stranded with the /hex command while a Booster Cookie is active.
- Added the following items to Villager trades:
  - Small Pocket Black Hole: 16 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Medium Pocket Black Hole: 2 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Soul String: 32 Emeralds, T2 Villager Trade
  - Ancient Claw: 64 Emeralds for 16x, T3 Villager Trade
  - Enchanted Ancient Claw: 2 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Recomboulator 3000: 10 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Challenging Rod: 32 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Legend Rod: 4 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Rod of the Sea: 16 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
  - Magma Rod: 32 Emeralds, T1 Villager Trade
  - Inferno Rod: 4 Enchanted Emeralds, T3 Villager Trade
  - Hellfire Rod: 16 Enchanted Emeralds, T5 Villager Trade
- Added a /trade for Oak Saplings for 250 coins per.
- Resync'd the Stranded Treasure Fishing loot pool with the regular loot pool.
  - Name Tags are still obtainable as a Good Catch, but will not be nearly as common as before.
- Significantly increased the obtainability of Golden/Diamond Goblins and Mithril/Gemstone Powder.
  - Increased the spawn chance of Golden/Diamond Goblins by 10x.
  - Increased the drop chance of Goblin Eggs dropped from eligible mobs by 2x.
  - Increased the powder drops of Golden/Diamond Goblins as follows:
    - Golden Goblin: 10,000 Mithril Powder
    - Diamond Goblin: 40,000 Mithril and Gemstone Powder
- Adjusted the following Villager item to emerald trades: (trade values may vary in practice as there's 11% variance implemented with villager trades)
  - Raw Mutton: 214 → 18 per emerald
  - Wool: 36 → 18 per emerald
  - Raw Rabbit: 33 → 9 per emerald
  - Wood (all types): 70 → 40 per emerald
  - Lapis Lazuli: 173 → 153 per emerald
  - Flint: 33 → 23 per emerald
  - Ghast Tear: 16 → 5 per emerald
  - Iron Ingot: 51 → 30 per emerald
  - Blaze Rod: 25 → 15 per emerald
  - Magma Cream: 46 → 25 per emerald
  - Slimeball: 59 → 25 per emerald
  - Glowstone Dust: 103 → 40 per emerald
  - Clay: 105 → 35 per emerald
  - Spider Eye: 118 → 27 per emerald
  - Coal: 60 → 30 per emerald
  - Gold Ingot: 40 → 25 per emerald
  - Gunpowder: 31 → 20 per emerald
  - Diamond: 29 → 20 per emerald
  - Nether Quartz: 38 → 15 per emerald
  - Ender Pearl: 26 → 15 per emerald
  - End Stone: 33 → 20 per emerald
  - Redstone: 120 → 88 per emerald
  - Bone: 33 → 22 per emerald
- Fixed being unable to obtain the Mosquito Pet and Mushroom Armor on Stranded.
- Removed the HoTM requirement for Beacons on Stranded.
- The HoTM task in the SkyBlock guide now displays Powder SBXP progress.
- There is now an End of Life indicator/warning when creating a new Stranded profile and hovering over your Stranded profile in the profile selector.
## ► QOL Changes
Today we have a number of high-impact quality-of-life changes to several areas of the game.
### ➥ Combat
- Adjusted the unlock order of the Kuudra Pet's perks. The new order can be found below:
  - COMMON -> Wither Bait
  - UNCOMMON -> Trophy Bait
  - RARE -> Crimson
  - EPIC -> Kuudra Fortune
  - LEGENDARY -> Kuudra Specialist
    - [!] Designer Note: A key goal here is for the "Trophy Bait" perk to be more accessible, since +20 Trophy Chance is a massive amount to be locked behind the Epic tier of the pet.
- Adjusted the rarity of the Wolf Talisman from COMMON -> UNCOMMON.
- Adjusted the purchase requirements for the Blood God Crest:
  - Can now be purchased infinitely instead of once per profile.
  - Now capped at +7 Strength.
- Rogue Swords can now be purchased at the Weaponsmith
### ➥ Dungeoneering
- Added the Spirit Mask to the Master Mode Floor 4 drop table and removed it from the Dark Auction.
  - Can only drop from the Obsidian Chest, costs 15m to open.
  - Has 4x less weight than a Spirit Sword.
- Added a chat message that displays whenever you level up a Boss Collection.
- Adjusted Golden Heads and Diamond Heads to now be purchased from Ophelia upon being unlocked.
  - [!] Designer Note: With this change, Diamond Heads now carry over upgrades from Golden Heads.
- Added 250 Gold Essence to Boss Collection tiers where Golden Heads were previously awarded.
- Added 1,000 Diamond Essence to Boss Collection tiers where Diamond Heads were previously awarded
- Adjusted the default dungeons class from Archer -> Berserk.
- Removed the ability to activate Berserk's Throwing Axe by sneaking while in The Catacombs.
### ➥ Fishing
- Added a Golden Fish Tablist Widget to the Crimson Isle.
- Added a Seal Pet to Lukas the Aquarist's shop during the Year of the Seal!
- Adjusted the speed of earlier-tier Fishing Minions to ensure the speed increase was more gradual between tiers.
- Adjusted Fishing Baits to only be consumed upon successful catch.
- Adjusted Sea Creatures to no longer attack players aside from the spawner unless provoked.
- Removed the mechanic that would cause you to catch nothing if you didn't move for a while when fishing.
### ➥ Miscellaneous
- Added the ability for players to retrieve perishable minion fuels (e.g Hyper Catalysts) from minions.
- Adjusted the icons used for certain stats in /stats
- Added reminder text to all Accessories to inform the player that they still function while in the Accessory Bag.
- Added the ability for Hoppity to take coins from Bank if you have insufficient funds in your Purse.
- Adjusted the Pig Pet, giving it a new set of perks.
- Adjusted items within the 20s can't-buy period on the Auction House to be hidden until the 20s has elapsed.
- Removed the ability to Recombobulate several types of items:
  - Wisp Upgrade Stones
  - Enchanted Bookshelves
  - Bejewelled Handles
  - Soul String
  - Griffin Feathers
- Removed NPC sell prices from several special items:
  - Creative Mind
  - Quality Map
  - Dead Bush of Love
  - Game Breaker
  - Game Fixer
  - French Bread
  - The Cake
## ► Bug Fixes
As always we've fixed some bugs with this release.
- Fixed Pets displaying the wrong way on latest versions
- Fixed Museum displays floating too high above their pedestals on latest versions
- Fixed certain collections being missing from the SkyBlock Guide
- Fixed being able to apply Pet Skins to hidden Pets
- Fixed Market Info in the /viewitem menu not showing the correct Auction House prices
- Fixed enemy Health sometimes being formatted incorrectly
- Fixed being unable to use the Lesser Orb of Healing while on the Farming Islands
- Fixed Phoenix Pet and Spirit Mask respawn location inconsistencies with other death types in the Garden
## ► What's Next?
We'll have a sneak peak thread up for a new farming event that'll happen every SkyBlock Year soon.
some letters are &o
That's all for today!
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!

# Changelog/2026/February 4
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[February 4] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: February 4, 2026
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
- Added an offline grace period for mutations that spread while the player is offline:
  - First, 3 growth cycles pass normally. After that, a 5 cycle grace period begins, in which they don't grow and don't consume water. If the player hasn't logged on by the end of that grace period, the mutations resume normal growth cycles.
- Breaking ungrown mutations that would not yield any drops now requires a confirmation (which can be toggled off)
- Fixed double quantity in pest rare drops message
- Gave Zombud and Godseed priority over other crops in mutating
- Fixed Garden Chip SkyBlock XP tasks not showing up in the SkyBlock Guide
- Fixed more NPCs not accepting Bachelor's Roses sometimes
- Fixed Watering Cans not carrying over data when upgrading at Farming Essentials in SkyMart
- Fixed crops dropping while clearing an uncleared plot
- Fixed Crop Fever Wart "Pray to RNGESUS" level dropping 2 instead of 6
- Fixed Spooky Bait not mentioning Spooky Festival Requirement in lore
- Fixed Alchemage Visitor requirement message
- Fixed being able to apply multiple Etherwarp Conduits to the Aspect of the End
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are still balancing and patching this release. This list is not exhaustive and is subject to change.

# Changelog/2026/January 12
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[Jan 12] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: January 12, 2026
Howdy y'all! I've got a list of fairly significant changes and fixes that just rolled to the game:
Changelog:
- Removed the Soul Eater damage cap while the player is in Dungeons, and reverted the damage change from 0.23.5.
  - The enchant description should be more clear as to its behavior now.
- Lowered the damage cap of Burning Vengeance to 25m, and made the behavior more consistent to prevent dealing that damage multiple times.
- Fixed an issue where Pest Traps would refill themselves on collection when you hadn't collected them in a long time.
- Fixed an issue with the Enderman Pet's 'Teleport Savvy' perk sustaining when the pet was unequipped.
- Fixed an issue with the Diana skin model.
- Fixed some issues with legacy reforges still having stats on Mythic equipment.
- Fixed an issue where Cocooning could give rewards multiple times under special conditions.
  - This update also re-enables cocooning.
That's all! If you have any bugs with this release (or others) that you'd like to report, please report them HERE!!

# Changelog/2026/January 19
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Dark Auction Server Patch
Updated: January 19, 2026
Hello,
Today we’ve reduced Dark Auction server size from 20 players to 12. We also changed the minimum player-count in Dark Auctions from 10 to 5.
Fewer players per server means less overbidding and more players actually walking away with items.
We’ll keep an eye on how this impacts prices and availability.
That’s it for now!
